  plan_title varchar [note: 'Descriptive title of the implementation plan, summarizing the intervention scope and geographic focus.']
  plan_type varchar [note: 'Classification of the implementation plan by temporal scope and purpose (annual work plan, quarterly plan, emergency response plan, etc.).']
  plan_version varchar [note: 'Version number of the implementation plan following semantic versioning (e.g., 1.0, 1.1, 2.0) to track revisions and amendments.']
  planning_period_end_date date [note: 'End date of the planning period covered by this implementation plan, marking the scheduled completion of all activities.']
  planning_period_start_date date [note: 'Start date of the planning period covered by this implementation plan, marking the beginning of scheduled activities.']
  reporting_frequency varchar [note: 'Frequency at which progress reports on this implementation plan will be generated for internal management and donor reporting. [ENUM-REF-CANDIDATE: weekly|biweekly|monthly|quarterly|semi_annual|annual|ad_hoc — 7 candidates stripped; promote to reference product]']
  resource_requirements_summary varchar [note: 'Summary of human resources, equipment, supplies, and other resources required to execute this implementation plan.']
  responsible_unit varchar [note: 'Name of the organizational unit, department, or country office responsible for executing this implementation plan.']
  revision_reason varchar [note: 'Explanation of why the implementation plan was revised, including changes in context, donor requirements, or operational constraints.']
  risk_level varchar [note: 'Overall risk level assessment for successful execution of this implementation plan, considering security, access, capacity, and external factors.']
  risk_mitigation_summary varchar [note: 'Summary of key risks identified and mitigation strategies planned to ensure implementation plan success.']
  sector_classification varchar [note: 'Primary humanitarian or development sector that this implementation plan addresses, aligned with cluster coordination system. [ENUM-REF-CANDIDATE: WASH|health|nutrition|education|protection|livelihoods|shelter|food_security|multi_sector — 9 candidates stripped; promote to reference product]']
  target_beneficiary_count varchar [note: 'Total number of direct beneficiaries (individuals or households) targeted by this implementation plan.']
  target_beneficiary_type varchar [note: 'Unit of measurement for target beneficiaries (individuals, households, communities, or institutions).']
  total_planned_activities varchar [note: 'Total number of discrete activities scheduled in this implementation plan.']
  total_planned_milestones varchar [note: 'Total number of key milestones defined in this implementation plan for progress tracking and donor reporting.']

  Note: 'Detailed implementation plan (work plan) for a program intervention or component, serving as the operational roadmap for field teams and the baseline for progress tracking. Captures planned activities at the task level including activity name, description, type, responsible parties, timelines (Gantt-style), resource requirements, geographic phasing, target beneficiary counts, status tracking, and linkage to LogFrame outputs. Includes comprehensive milestone tracking (program launch, mid-term review, final evaluation, closeout, donor reporting deadlines) with milestone type, planned/actual dates, completion status, deliverable description, sign-off authority, and dependency sequencing. Stores plan version, planning period, approval status, and last revision date. This is the SSOT for all program scheduling, activity sequencing, and milestone management — the schedule authority for donor reporting timelines and grant compliance.'
}

Table program.budget_plan {
  budget_plan_id varchar [pk, note: 'Unique identifier for the program budget plan record.']
  component_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to program.component. Business justification: In SAP S/4HANA programme accounting and NGO programme management, budget plans are often structured at the component/workstream level — each component has its own budget envelope. Adding component_id ']
  intervention_id varchar [note: 'Reference to the program intervention this budget plan supports.']
  regulatory_filing_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to compliance.regulatory_filing. Business justification: Budget plans are submitted as part of regulatory filings (Form 990 Schedule I, single audit schedules, donor financial reports). Direct operational link for nonprofit financial compliance reporting.']
  amendment_date date [note: 'Date on which this budget plan amendment was executed.']
  amendment_reason varchar [note: 'Explanation of why this budget plan was amended or revised, if applicable.']
  approval_date date [note: 'Date on which this budget plan was formally approved.']
  budget_assumptions decimal(18,2) [note: 'Key assumptions underlying the budget plan, such as exchange rates, inflation rates, beneficiary numbers, or activity volumes.']
  budget_narrative decimal(18,2) [note: 'Detailed narrative justification and explanation of the budget plan, including assumptions, methodologies, and cost calculations.']
  budget_owner decimal(18,2) [note: 'Name or identifier of the program manager or budget holder responsible for this budget plan.']
  budget_period_end_date date [note: 'End date of the budget period this plan covers.']
  budget_period_start_date date [note: 'Start date of the budget period this plan covers.']
  budget_plan_number decimal(18,2) [note: 'Externally-known unique identifier or code for this budget plan, used in donor reporting and financial communications.']
  budget_status decimal(18,2) [note: 'Current lifecycle status of the budget plan in the approval and execution workflow. [ENUM-REF-CANDIDATE: draft|submitted|under_review|approved|active|amended|closed|rejected — 8 candidates stripped; promote to reference product]']
  budget_type decimal(18,2) [note: 'Classification of the budget plan indicating whether it is the original approved budget, a revision, supplemental funding, or closeout budget.']
  budget_version_number decimal(18,2) [note: 'Version number of this budget plan, incremented with each revision or amendment.']
  compliance_notes varchar [note: 'Notes regarding compliance requirements, restrictions, or special conditions attached to this budget plan.']
  contractual_costs decimal(18,2) [note: 'Total budgeted amount for contractual services, consultants, and sub-awards.']
  cost_share_amount decimal(18,2) [note: 'Total budgeted amount of cost sharing or matching funds contributed by the organization or other sources.']
  created_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Timestamp when this budget plan record was first created in the system.']
  currency_code varchar [note: 'Three-letter ISO 4217 currency code for all monetary amounts in this budget plan.']
  dac_sector_code varchar [note: 'OECD DAC sector classification code for this budget plan, used for international aid transparency and reporting.']
  donor_budget_code decimal(18,2) [note: 'Donor-specific budget classification code or identifier required for reporting and compliance.']
  donor_visibility_flag boolean [note: 'Indicates whether this budget plan is visible to the donor in reporting portals or dashboards.']
  equipment_costs decimal(18,2) [note: 'Total budgeted amount for equipment purchases with unit cost exceeding capitalization threshold.']
  finance_contact varchar [note: 'Name or identifier of the finance team member responsible for financial oversight of this budget plan.']
  fringe_benefits_costs decimal(18,2) [note: 'Total budgeted amount for fringe benefits associated with personnel costs.']
  indirect_cost_rate decimal(5,2) [note: 'Approved indirect cost rate percentage applied to the direct cost base, as negotiated in NICRA or per de minimis rate.']
  indirect_costs decimal(18,2) [note: 'Total budgeted amount for indirect costs (Facilities and Administration), calculated by applying the indirect cost rate to the direct cost base.']
  last_modified_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Timestamp when this budget plan record was last updated or modified.']
  name decimal(18,2) [note: 'Descriptive name or title of the budget plan, typically aligned with program or grant phase.']
  notes varchar [note: 'Additional free-text notes or comments about this budget plan.']
  personnel_costs decimal(18,2) [note: 'Total budgeted amount for personnel salaries and wages.']
  sdg_alignment varchar [note: 'Sustainable Development Goal(s) this budget plan contributes to, typically represented as SDG numbers (e.g., SDG 1, SDG 3).']
  sector_classification varchar [note: 'Humanitarian or development sector classification for this budget plan. [ENUM-REF-CANDIDATE: wash|health|nutrition|education|protection|livelihoods|shelter|food_security|emergency_response|capacity_building — 10 candidates stripped; promote to reference product]']
  submitted_date date [note: 'Date on which this budget plan was submitted for approval or to the donor.']
  supplies_costs decimal(18,2) [note: 'Total budgeted amount for supplies and consumable materials.']
  total_approved_budget_amount decimal(18,2) [note: 'Total approved budget amount for this plan, representing the sum of all cost categories.']
  total_direct_costs decimal(18,2) [note: 'Sum of all direct cost categories before indirect costs are applied.']
  travel_costs decimal(18,2) [note: 'Total budgeted amount for travel expenses including international and domestic travel.']

  Note: 'Program-level budget plan capturing the approved budget envelope for a program intervention, including line-item detail by cost category (personnel, fringe, travel, equipment, supplies, contractual, other direct costs, indirect costs). Stores total approved budget, currency, budget period, budget version, donor-specific budget codes, indirect cost rate (ICR/NICRA), F&A allocation, unit costs, quantities, and budget status. Distinct from the finance domain\'s GL and BvA tracking — this is the program-level planning budget owned by program management.'
}

Table program.budget_plan_line {
  budget_plan_line_id varchar [pk, note: 'Unique identifier for the budget plan line item. Primary key for this entity.']

Business justification: Individual budget line items in partner-executed programs must trace to the partnership agreement for line-item allowability verification, indirect cost rate application, cost-sharing validation, and ']
  award_id varchar [note: 'Reference to the grant or funding award that finances this budget line. Enables donor-specific budget reporting and compliance tracking.']
  budget_plan_id varchar [note: 'Reference to the parent budget plan document that contains this line item. Links to the overall program budget structure.']
  component_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to program.component. Business justification: Individual budget line items in NGO programme budgets are allocated to specific components/workstreams for cost attribution and donor reporting. Adding component_id to budget_plan_line enables compone']
  fund_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to donor.fund. Business justification: Individual budget line items allocated to specific donor funds enable granular restriction tracking and line-item fund accounting. Required for detailed financial reporting, multi-fund budget allocati']
  intervention_id varchar [note: 'Reference to the program or project to which this budget line belongs. Enables program-level budget rollup and tracking.']
  logframe_row_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to program.logframe_row. Business justification: Results-based management (RBM) in UN/NGO programmes requires direct linkage between budget line items and logframe results — each budget line should be attributable to a specific output, outcome, or a']
  activity_code varchar [note: 'Program activity or intervention code that this budget line supports. Links budget to program implementation activities.']
  allowable_cost_flag boolean [note: 'Indicates whether this cost is allowable under the grant or donor regulations. Used for budget review and compliance validation.']
  approval_date date [note: 'Date when this budget line was formally approved. Critical for compliance and audit documentation.']
  approval_required_flag boolean [note: 'Indicates whether this budget line requires explicit donor or management approval before execution. Used for budget control and governance.']
  budget_period_end_date date [note: 'The end date of the budget period for which this line item is planned. Defines the timeframe for budget execution and BvA analysis.']
  budget_period_start_date date [note: 'The start date of the budget period for which this line item is planned. Enables multi-year and phased budget tracking.']
  budget_plan_line_status decimal(18,2) [note: 'Current lifecycle status of the budget line item. Tracks approval workflow and execution state. [ENUM-REF-CANDIDATE: draft|submitted|approved|active|revised|closed|cancelled — 7 candidates stripped; promote to reference product]']
  budget_version_number decimal(18,2) [note: 'Version number of the budget plan to track revisions and amendments. Incremented with each budget modification.']

  cost_category varchar [note: 'Primary budget classification category following standard nonprofit and federal grant budget structures. Used for donor reporting and financial compliance. [ENUM-REF-CANDIDATE: personnel|fringe_benefits|travel|equipment|supplies|contractual|subawards|other_direct_costs|indirect_costs — 9 candidates stripped; promote to reference product]']
  cost_sharing_amount decimal(18,2) [note: 'The portion of the total planned amount that represents cost sharing or matching contribution. Used for grant compliance reporting.']
  cost_sharing_flag boolean [note: 'Indicates whether this budget line includes cost sharing or matching funds from the organization or other sources. Critical for grant compliance.']
  cost_sharing_source varchar [note: 'The source of cost sharing funds (e.g., organizational reserves, other grants, in-kind contributions, partner contributions).']
  cost_subcategory varchar [note: 'Detailed subcategory within the primary cost category for granular budget tracking (e.g., international travel, local travel, office supplies, medical supplies).']
  created_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Date and time when this budget line record was first created in the system. Required for audit trail and data lineage.']
  currency_code varchar [note: 'Three-letter ISO 4217 currency code for the budget line amounts (e.g., USD, EUR, GBP). Enables multi-currency budget management.']
  dac_sector_code varchar [note: 'OECD DAC 5-digit sector classification code for international development reporting and sector-based budget analysis.']
  direct_cost_flag boolean [note: 'Indicates whether this budget line is a direct cost (true) or an indirect/overhead cost (false). Used for indirect cost rate calculations and compliance.']
  donor_budget_code decimal(18,2) [note: 'Donor-specific budget line code or reference number required for grant reporting and financial compliance. Maps to donor financial systems.']
  fiscal_year varchar [note: 'The fiscal year to which this budget line belongs. Enables fiscal year-based budget reporting and analysis.']
  indirect_cost_rate decimal(5,4) [note: 'The indirect cost rate applied to this budget line if it is a direct cost base. Expressed as a decimal (e.g., 0.1500 for 15%). Links to NICRA.']
  last_modified_by varchar [note: 'Identifier of the user or system that last modified this budget line record. Supports change tracking and accountability.']
  last_modified_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Date and time when this budget line record was last updated. Critical for audit trail and version control.']
  line_description varchar [note: 'Detailed narrative description of the budget line item explaining the purpose, justification, and planned use of funds.']
  line_number varchar [note: 'Sequential or hierarchical line number within the budget plan for ordering and reference purposes (e.g., 1.1, 1.2, 2.1).']
  notes varchar [note: 'Additional notes, comments, or clarifications about this budget line item for internal reference and donor communication.']
  quantity decimal(18,4) [note: 'The number of units planned for this budget line item. Used with unit cost to calculate total planned amount.']
  revision_reason varchar [note: 'Explanation for any revision or amendment to this budget line. Required for donor reporting and audit trail.']
  sdg_alignment varchar [note: 'United Nations Sustainable Development Goal number(s) that this budget line contributes to (e.g., SDG 1, SDG 3, SDG 6). Supports impact reporting.']
  total_planned_amount decimal(18,2) [note: 'The total budgeted amount for this line item, typically calculated as quantity multiplied by unit cost. Primary financial value for budget versus actual (BvA) tracking.']
  unit_cost decimal(18,2) [note: 'The cost per single unit of measure. Multiplied by quantity to derive the total planned amount for this line.']
  unit_of_measure varchar [note: 'The unit by which the budget item is measured (e.g., person-months, trips, units, days, hours, items, sessions).']
  created_by varchar [note: 'Identifier of the user or system that created this budget line record. Supports audit trail and data governance.']

  Note: 'Individual budget line item within a program budget, capturing cost category (personnel, fringe, travel, equipment, supplies, contractual, other direct costs, indirect costs), budget line description, unit cost, quantity, unit of measure, total planned amount, donor budget code, and cost-sharing flag. Enables granular budget management, BvA tracking linkage to finance domain, and donor financial reporting by budget category.'
}

Table program.program_partnership {
  program_partnership_id varchar [pk, note: 'Primary key for the partnership association']
  component_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to program.component. Business justification: In NGO programme delivery, implementing partners are often assigned to specific components/workstreams of an intervention — a partner may implement Component 1 (WASH) while another implements Componen']
  fund_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to donor.donor_fund. Business justification: Sub-award donor compliance reporting requires linking each implementing partner sub-award to the earmarked donor fund financing it. Donor audits and IATI reporting require fund-level traceability to p']
  partner_org_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to the partner organization participating in this intervention']
  project_site_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to field.project_site. Business justification: Partner organizations are accountable for activities at specific project sites under sub-award agreements. NGO sub-award management and partner performance monitoring require knowing which site a part']
  beneficiary_target_count varchar [note: 'Number of beneficiaries this partner is responsible for reaching within this intervention. Sum of all partner beneficiary targets should equal the intervention\'s total target beneficiary count.']
  budget_allocated_amount decimal(18,2) [note: 'Total budget amount allocated to this partner for their scope of work in this intervention. Sum of all partner budget allocations should equal or be less than the intervention\'s total budget.']
  capacity_assessment_status varchar [note: 'Result of the capacity assessment conducted specifically for this partner\'s role in this intervention. May differ from the partner\'s general organizational capacity assessment.']
  compliance_status varchar [note: 'Current compliance status of this partner for this intervention against contractual obligations, donor regulations, and organizational policies. Tracks whether the partner is meeting reporting, financial, and programmatic requirements.']
  end_date date [note: 'Date when this partner completed or will complete their scope of work for this intervention. May differ from the intervention\'s overall end date if partners have staggered timelines.']
  geographic_scope varchar [note: 'Specific geographic area (region, district, or community) where this partner is responsible for implementing activities within the broader intervention geography. Allows multiple partners to cover different areas of the same intervention.']
  ip_transfer_modality varchar [note: 'HACT IP transfer modality: Direct Cash Transfer / Reimbursement / Direct Payment']
  partnership_role varchar [note: 'The specific role this partner plays in the intervention (e.g., Lead Implementer, Sub-Recipient, Technical Partner, Local CBO, Consortium Member). Defines the partner\'s primary responsibility and authority level.']
  partnership_status varchar [note: 'Current lifecycle status of this specific partnership arrangement within the intervention. Tracks whether the partner is actively implementing, suspended due to performance issues, or completed their scope of work.']
  partnership_type varchar [note: 'Classification of the partnership arrangement type (e.g., Direct Implementation, Sub-Award, Technical Assistance, Capacity Building, Coordination). Determines reporting and compliance requirements.']
  performance_rating varchar [note: 'Performance assessment rating for this partner\'s work on this specific intervention. Based on delivery against milestones, budget utilization, reporting quality, and beneficiary feedback.']
  reporting_frequency varchar [note: 'Required frequency for this partner to submit progress reports for this intervention. Determined by risk rating, budget size, and donor requirements.']
  risk_rating varchar [note: 'Risk level assigned to this specific partnership based on partner capacity, geographic context, budget size, and intervention complexity. Determines oversight and monitoring intensity.']
  sector_focus varchar [note: 'Specific sector or sub-sector within the intervention that this partner is responsible for. Relevant for multi-sectoral interventions where different partners handle different technical areas (e.g., one partner for WASH, another for nutrition).']
  start_date date [note: 'Date when this partner began implementation activities for this intervention. May differ from the intervention\'s overall start date if partners were phased in.']

  Note: 'This association product represents the formal partnership agreement between a humanitarian/development intervention and a partner organization. It captures the specific role, responsibilities, budget allocation, performance metrics, and compliance status for each partner\'s involvement in each intervention. Each record links one intervention to one partner organization with attributes that exist only in the context of this specific partnership arrangement.. Existence Justification: In nonprofit humanitarian operations, interventions are routinely implemented through multi-partner consortia where a single intervention engages multiple partner organizations (lead implementer, sub-recipients, local CBOs, technical specialists) each with distinct roles, geographic coverage, and budget allocations. Simultaneously, established partner organizations implement multiple interventions across different sectors and geographies. The partnership arrangement itself is an operational business entity that program managers actively create, monitor, and close out, with specific attributes like role, budget allocation, performance rating, compliance status, and risk assessment that belong to neither the intervention nor the partner organization alone.'
}

  region varchar [note: 'Geographic region or administrative area within the country where the program operates.']
  reporting_frequency varchar [note: 'Required frequency for submitting progress and financial reports to donors and stakeholders.']
  risk_rating varchar [note: 'Overall risk assessment level for the program considering contextual, operational, financial, and reputational risks.']
  sdg_alignment varchar [note: 'Comma-separated list of SDG goal numbers (1-17) and targets that the program contributes to.']
  sector_code varchar [note: 'Five-digit code identifying the primary humanitarian or development sector (WASH, Health, Nutrition, Education, Protection, Livelihood, Shelter, etc.).']
  sector_name varchar [note: 'Human-readable name of the primary sector in which the program operates.']
  start_date date [note: 'Official start date when program implementation begins as per the approved proposal or agreement.']
  subsector varchar [note: 'More granular classification within the primary sector (e.g., Water Supply, Sanitation, Hygiene Promotion under WASH).']
  target_beneficiaries varchar [note: 'Planned total number of direct beneficiaries the program aims to reach.']
  target_population varchar [note: 'Description of the intended beneficiary population including demographic characteristics, vulnerability criteria, and geographic scope.']
  theory_of_change varchar [note: 'Narrative or structured description of the causal pathway linking program inputs, activities, outputs, outcomes, and impact.']
  created_by varchar [note: 'Username or identifier of the user who created the program record.']

  Note: 'Master reference table for program. Referenced by program_id. Systems-of-record: eTools, InSight, SAP PS (Project System). IATI Organisation Standard alignment. Links to CPD/UNSDCF outcomes.'
}

Table grant.award {
  award_id varchar [pk, note: 'Primary key for award record']
  constituent_id varchar [note: 'FK to the donor constituent']
  country_office_id varchar [note: 'FK to the managing country office']
  fund_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to donor.donor_fund. Business justification: An award is funded through a specific donor fund. Linking award to donor_fund enables fund utilization tracking, restriction compliance monitoring, and fund balance reconciliation — essential for NGO ']
  funding_source_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to grant.funding_source. Business justification: An award is issued under a specific funding source (donor mechanism such as USAID cooperative agreement, EC grant, UNICEF HACT). This FK normalizes the relationship between the award lifecycle and the']
  intervention_id varchar [note: 'FK to the program intervention funded']
  psea_policy_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to safeguarding.psea_policy. [ENUM-REF-CANDIDATE: individual|institutional|foundation|corporate|bilateral|multilateral|household — 7 candidates stripped; promote to reference product]']

Geographic interest alignment, relationship ownership, and prospect pipeline reporting by country office are standard major gifts management pro']
  ability_score varchar [note: 'Ability score component of the LAI (Linkage-Ability-Interest) assessment. Measures financial capacity to make a significant gift. Typically scored 1-5.']

  created_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Timestamp when this prospect record was first created in the system. Audit trail field for data governance.']

  disqualification_reason varchar [note: 'Explanation for why the prospect was disqualified from active cultivation. Null if prospect is not disqualified. Examples: \'Insufficient capacity\', \'No mission alignment\', \'Unresponsive to outreach\', \'Ethical concerns\'.']
  donor_recognition_preference varchar [note: 'Prospect\'s stated preference for public recognition of their giving. Public (full recognition in donor lists and materials), Anonymous (no public attribution), Limited (recognition in select contexts only), No Preference (not yet specified).']
  email_address varchar [note: 'Primary email address for prospect communication and correspondence.']
  estimated_capacity decimal(18,2) [note: 'Estimated total giving capacity of the prospect in USD. Based on wealth screening, research, and capacity assessment. For institutional donors, represents estimated annual grant-making budget available for alignment with organizational mission.']

  last_contact_date date [note: 'Date of the most recent meaningful contact or engagement with the prospect. Includes meetings, calls, events, correspondence.']
  last_contact_type varchar [note: 'Type of the most recent contact or engagement activity with the prospect. [ENUM-REF-CANDIDATE: meeting|phone_call|email|event|site_visit|proposal_submission|other — 7 candidates stripped; promote to reference product]']
  linkage_score varchar [note: 'Linkage score component of the LAI (Linkage-Ability-Interest) assessment. Measures strength of existing connections to the organization (board members, staff, volunteers, beneficiaries). Typically scored 1-5.']
  mailing_address varchar [note: 'Full mailing address for prospect correspondence. Includes street address, city, state/province, postal code, and country.']
  modified_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Timestamp when this prospect record was last modified. Audit trail field for data governance and change tracking.']
  name varchar [note: 'Full legal or preferred name of the prospective donor or funder. For institutional donors, this is the organization name.']
  next_action_date date [note: 'Scheduled date for the next cultivation activity or touchpoint with this prospect. Used for pipeline management and follow-up tracking.']
  next_action_description varchar [note: 'Description of the planned next cultivation action or engagement activity (e.g., \'Schedule site visit\', \'Send program impact report\', \'Invite to annual gala\', \'Submit grant proposal\').']
  phone_number varchar [note: 'Primary phone number for prospect contact. Includes country code for international prospects.']
  previous_giving_history varchar [note: 'Summary of the prospect\'s known philanthropic giving history to other organizations or prior gifts to this organization. Includes gift amounts, recipient organizations, and giving patterns.']
  probability_percentage decimal(5,2) [note: 'Estimated probability (0-100%) that this prospect will convert to an active donor and make a gift. Used for pipeline forecasting and weighted revenue projections.']
  program_interest_area varchar [note: 'Primary program area or thematic focus that aligns with the prospect\'s philanthropic interests (e.g., \'WASH\', \'Education\', \'Health\', \'Emergency Response\', \'Gender Equality\'). May reference SDG (Sustainable Development Goal) alignment.']
  prospect_status varchar [note: 'Current operational status of the prospect record. Active (currently being cultivated), Inactive (no current engagement), Disqualified (determined not viable), Converted (became active donor), On Hold (temporarily paused cultivation).']
  prospect_type varchar [note: 'Classification of the prospect by donor category. Individual (major gift prospects), Foundation (private/family/community foundations), Corporate (corporate sponsors), Institutional (USAID, DFID, DAC members), Government (bilateral government donors), Multilateral (UN agencies, World Bank, regional development banks).']
  qualification_date date [note: 'Date when the prospect was qualified as a viable cultivation target after initial research and assessment. Null if not yet qualified.']
  rating varchar [note: 'Qualitative rating assigned by prospect research or major gift officer indicating overall prospect quality and likelihood of conversion. A+ (highest priority, strongest capacity and affinity), D (lowest priority).']
  research_stage varchar [note: 'Current stage of prospect research activities. Not Started (no research conducted), Preliminary (initial screening and public records review), In Depth (comprehensive research including wealth screening and relationship mapping), Completed (research finalized and briefing prepared), Ongoing Monitoring (periodic updates and news monitoring).']
  solicitation_amount decimal(18,2) [note: 'Specific dollar amount being requested or proposed in the current solicitation. Null if not yet at solicitation stage.']
  solicitation_date date [note: 'Date when the formal ask or grant proposal was submitted to the prospect. Null if not yet solicited.']
  source_of_wealth varchar [note: 'Primary source of the prospect\'s wealth or funding capacity (e.g., \'Technology entrepreneur\', \'Inherited wealth\', \'Real estate\', \'Government appropriation\', \'Endowment income\'). For institutional donors, describes funding source.']
  stage varchar [note: 'Current stage in the donor cultivation pipeline lifecycle. Identification (newly identified prospect), Qualification (research and capacity assessment underway), Cultivation (relationship building and engagement), Solicitation (active proposal or ask), Negotiation (terms and agreement discussion), Stewardship (post-gift relationship management).']
  wealth_screening_score decimal(5,2) [note: 'Quantitative wealth screening score from third-party wealth screening service (e.g., WealthEngine, iWave). Typically scaled 0-100 indicating relative wealth capacity.']

  Note: 'Prospect research and donor cultivation pipeline record managed in Raisers Edge NXT and Salesforce Nonprofit Cloud. Tracks prospective donors and funders through the cultivation lifecycle from identification through qualification, cultivation, solicitation, and stewardship. Captures prospect rating, estimated giving capacity, wealth screening score, research stage, assigned major gift officer, next action date, cultivation strategy notes, and linkage-ability-interest (LAI) assessment. Supports the Donor Cultivation and Fundraising core business process.'
}

  major_gift_opportunity_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to donor.major_gift_opportunity. Business justification: Gifts can close major gift opportunities.   original_gift_id varchar [note: 'Reference to the original employee gift that this matching gift is matching. Populated only for matching gifts.']

Installment tracking, reminder schedules, and country-level pledge pipeline reporting are standard NGO finance processes. Country directors need v']
  donor_requirement_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to compliance.donor_requirement. Business justification: Pledge agreements with institutional funders carry specific donor requirements (branding, audit clauses, narrative reports). Linking pledge to donor_requirement enables compliance tracking of pledge-s']
  emergency_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to field.emergency. Business justification: Multi-year emergency response pledges are common in NGO fundraising. Linking pledges to emergencies enables emergency-level funding pipeline tracking, funding gap analysis against humanitarian respons']
  fund_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to donor.donor_fund. Business justification: A pledge commitment is designated to a specific donor fund. Without this FK, pledge fund designation is not formally linked to the authoritative donor_fund record. Adding pledge.donor_fund_id → donor_']

  major_gift_opportunity_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to donor.major_gift_opportunity. Linking pipeline to commitment enables conversion tracking and forecasting. Nu']
  obligation_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to compliance.obligation. Business justification: Institutional donor pledge agreements impose compliance obligations (restricted-use reporting schedules, matching conditions). NGO compliance officers track which obligation governs each pledge to ens']
  regulatory_filing_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to compliance.regulatory_filing. Business justification: Non-cash pledges and large pledge commitments trigger IRS and state regulatory filings (e.g., Form 8282, charitable solicitation filings). NGO compliance teams must link each pledge to its governing r']
  acknowledgment_date date [note: 'The date on which the pledge acknowledgment was sent to the donor.']
  acknowledgment_sent boolean [note: 'Indicates whether a pledge acknowledgment letter or receipt has been sent to the donor.']
  amount_paid decimal(18,2) [note: 'The cumulative amount paid to date across all installments. Updated as installment payments are received.']
  balance_outstanding decimal(18,2) [note: 'The remaining unpaid balance on the pledge. Calculated as total_pledge_amount minus amount_paid.']
  cancellation_date date [note: 'The date on which the donor or organization cancelled the pledge commitment. Populated only when pledge_status is cancelled.']
  cancellation_reason varchar [note: 'Free-text explanation for why the pledge was cancelled (e.g., donor request, duplicate entry, data entry error).']
  created_timestamp timestamp [note: 'The timestamp when this pledge record was first created in the system.']
  currency_code varchar [note: 'Three-letter ISO 4217 currency code for the pledge amount (e.g., USD, EUR, GBP).']
  first_installment_date date [note: 'The scheduled due date for the first installment payment.']
  fulfillment_date date [note: 'The date on which the pledge was fully paid and marked as fulfilled. Populated only when pledge_status is fulfilled.']
  installment_frequency varchar [note: 'The recurring schedule for installment payments. One-time for single-payment pledges; monthly, quarterly, semi-annual, annual for standard recurring schedules; custom for irregular or donor-specified schedules.']
  installments_paid varchar [note: 'The count of installments that have been fully paid to date. Updated as installment payments are received.']
  installments_remaining varchar [note: 'The count of installments that are still unpaid. Calculated as number_of_installments minus installments_paid.']
  is_anonymous boolean [note: 'Indicates whether the donor has requested that this pledge be kept anonymous in public recognition and reporting.']
  is_matching_gift_eligible boolean [note: 'Indicates whether this pledge is eligible for corporate matching gift programs.']
  is_recurring boolean [note: 'Indicates whether this pledge is a recurring sustainer commitment with ongoing installments beyond the initial schedule.']
  last_installment_date date [note: 'The scheduled due date for the final installment payment.']

  last_payment_date date [note: 'The date on which the most recent installment payment was received.']
  modified_timestamp timestamp [note: 'The timestamp when this pledge record was last modified in the system.']
  next_installment_amount decimal(18,2) [note: 'The scheduled amount for the next unpaid installment.']
  next_installment_due_date date [note: 'The due date for the next unpaid installment. Used for reminder generation and overdue tracking.']
  notes varchar [note: 'Free-text notes or comments about the pledge, including special donor instructions, stewardship considerations, or internal processing notes.']
  number_of_installments varchar [note: 'The total number of scheduled installment payments for this pledge. For one-time pledges, this is 1.']
  payment_method decimal(18,2) [note: 'The primary payment instrument or method the donor intends to use for installment payments.']
  pledge_date date [note: 'The date on which the donor made the pledge commitment. This is the principal business event timestamp for the pledge.']
  pledge_number varchar [note: 'Human-readable business identifier for the pledge commitment, often displayed on donor communications and receipts.']
  pledge_status varchar [note: 'Current lifecycle status of the pledge commitment. Active indicates ongoing installment schedule; fulfilled indicates all installments paid; lapsed indicates overdue with no recent payment; written-off indicates uncollectible; cancelled indicates donor withdrew commitment; pending indicates awaiting approval or activation.']
  pledge_type varchar [note: 'Classification of the pledge based on its purpose and structure. Standard for general multi-installment pledges; sustainer for recurring monthly giving; major gift for high-value commitments; planned giving for estate or deferred gifts; capital campaign for infrastructure projects; endowment for permanent fund contributions.']
  reminder_schedule varchar [note: 'The timing for automated payment reminders sent to the donor before each installment due date.']
  total_pledge_amount decimal(18,2) [note: 'The total monetary value committed by the donor across all installments. This is the gross pledge amount before any payments.']
  write_off_amount decimal(18,2) [note: 'The outstanding balance amount that was written off. Populated only when pledge_status is written_off.']
  write_off_date date [note: 'The date on which the pledge was written off as uncollectible. Populated only when pledge_status is written_off.']
  write_off_reason varchar [note: 'Free-text explanation for why the pledge was written off (e.g., donor deceased, donor financial hardship, unable to contact, donor dispute).']

  Note: 'Tracks multi-installment giving commitments, pledge agreements, and their individual installment schedules from donors, managed in Raisers Edge NXT and Salesforce Nonprofit Cloud. Captures pledge date, total pledge amount, installment schedule (monthly, quarterly, annual), number of installments, pledge balance outstanding, pledge status (active, fulfilled, lapsed, written-off), fund designation, campaign, appeal, reminder schedule, and write-off reason. Includes installment-level detail: individual due dates, scheduled amounts, actual payment dates, amounts paid, installment status (scheduled, paid, overdue, skipped, waived), payment method, and linked gift reference upon payment. Enables granular pledge fulfillment tracking, overdue installment identification, automated reminder generation, and BvA (Budget vs. Actual) reconciliation for pledged revenue. Distinct from gift — a pledge is a commitment to give, not a completed transaction.'
}

  fund_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to donor.donor_fund. Business justification: A major gift opportunity targets a specific fund designation. The major_gift_opportunity has gift_purpose and restriction_type as string fields but no formal FK to the authoritative donor_fund record.']

  actual_close_date date [note: 'Actual date when the opportunity was closed (won or lost). Populated when solicitation_stage reaches closed_won or closed_lost.']
  ask_amount decimal(18,2) [note: 'The specific dollar amount being requested from the donor in this solicitation. Represents the formal ask figure.']
  ask_strategy varchar [note: 'Narrative description of the solicitation approach, cultivation plan, and key messaging tailored to this donor\'s interests and capacity.']

  gift_purpose varchar [note: 'Detailed description of the intended use or program impact of the gift, aligned with donor interests and organizational priorities.']
  gift_type varchar [note: 'Classification of the expected gift instrument or vehicle (e.g., outright cash, multi-year pledge, bequest, stock transfer).']
  is_active boolean [note: 'Flag indicating whether this opportunity is currently active in the pipeline. False for closed opportunities or those marked inactive.']
  is_anonymous boolean [note: 'Flag indicating whether the donor has requested anonymity for this gift. True if donor wishes to remain anonymous in public recognition.']
  is_matching_gift_eligible boolean [note: 'Flag indicating whether this gift is eligible for corporate matching gift programs through the donor\'s employer.']
  last_contact_date date [note: 'Date of the most recent substantive interaction or touchpoint with the donor regarding this opportunity.']
  last_modified_date timestamp [note: 'Timestamp when this opportunity record was last updated or modified.']
  loss_reason varchar [note: 'Explanation or categorization of why the opportunity was closed-lost, if applicable. Used for pipeline analysis and strategy refinement.']
  next_step_action varchar [note: 'Description of the next planned cultivation or solicitation activity (e.g., site visit, proposal submission, follow-up meeting).']
  next_step_date date [note: 'Scheduled date for the next cultivation or solicitation activity with this prospect.']
  notes varchar [note: 'Confidential internal notes and observations about the opportunity, donor relationship dynamics, cultivation history, and strategic considerations.']

  opportunity_name varchar [note: 'Descriptive name or title for this major gift opportunity, typically including donor name and purpose (e.g., \'Smith Foundation - WASH Program Endowment\').']
  probability_percentage decimal(5,2) [note: 'Estimated likelihood (0-100%) that this opportunity will close successfully, based on donor engagement, capacity, and stage progression.']
  restriction_type varchar [note: 'Classification of donor-imposed restrictions on the gift per FASB ASC 958 standards for not-for-profit accounting.']
  solicitation_stage varchar [note: 'Current stage in the major gift solicitation pipeline. Tracks progression from initial identification through cultivation, ask, and closure. [ENUM-REF-CANDIDATE: identification|qualification|cultivation|solicitation|negotiation|stewardship|closed_won|closed_lost — 8 candidates stripped; promote to reference product]']
  source_channel varchar [note: 'The channel or method through which this major gift opportunity was originally identified or sourced. [ENUM-REF-CANDIDATE: direct_mail|event|peer_referral|board_introduction|prospect_research|planned_giving|online — 7 candidates stripped; promote to reference product]']
  stage_changed_date timestamp [note: 'Timestamp when the solicitation_stage was last changed, used to track velocity through the pipeline.']
  weighted_value decimal(18,2) [note: 'Calculated forecast value (expected_gift_amount × probability_percentage) used for revenue forecasting and pipeline analysis.']

  Note: 'Pipeline management record for major gift solicitations tracked in Raisers Edge NXT and Salesforce Nonprofit Cloud. Represents a specific ask or solicitation opportunity with a named prospect or donor. Captures opportunity name, ask amount, expected gift amount, probability percentage, solicitation stage (identification, qualification, cultivation, solicitation, stewardship, closed-won, closed-lost), expected close date, assigned major gift officer, fund designation, campaign, proposal reference, and ask strategy. Enables major gift pipeline reporting and revenue forecasting.'
}

  indicator_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to mel.indicator. Business justification: Fundraising appeals are designed around specific program indicators (e.g., \'Help us vaccinate 5,000 children\'). Linking appeal to indicator enables impact-based fundraising reporting, ROI analysis per']
  obligation_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to compliance.obligation. Business justification: Charitable solicitation obligations (state registration renewals, disclosure requirements) govern when and how appeals can be launched. Linking appeal to obligation enables compliance officers to bloc']
  regulatory_filing_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to compliance.regulatory_filing. Business justification: Charitable solicitation appeals require state-level regulatory filings before launch in most US jurisdictions. NGO compliance teams must link each appeal to its governing regulatory filing to confirm ']
  acknowledgment_template varchar [note: 'Name or identifier of the gift acknowledgment letter template used for donors responding to this appeal.']
  appeal_status varchar [note: 'Current lifecycle status of the appeal.']
  appeal_type varchar [note: 'Classification of the appeal based on donor lifecycle stage and solicitation strategy.']
  ask_amount decimal(15,2) [note: 'Suggested or recommended gift amount presented to constituents in the appeal solicitation.']
  ask_string varchar [note: 'Formatted ask string presented to constituents showing multiple giving levels (e.g., $50, $100, $250, $500, Other).']
  average_gift_amount decimal(15,2) [note: 'Average gift size for responses to this appeal calculated as total_revenue_amount / response_count.']
  channel varchar [note: 'Primary communication channel through which the appeal is delivered to constituents. [ENUM-REF-CANDIDATE: direct_mail|email|phone|digital|event|face_to_face|social_media — 7 candidates stripped; promote to reference product]']
  code varchar [note: 'Externally-known unique code for the appeal used in gift attribution and tracking (e.g., DM2024Q1, EMAIL-SPRING, PHONE-EOY).']

  modified_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Date and time when the appeal record was last modified in the system.']
  name varchar [note: 'Human-readable name of the appeal (e.g., Spring Direct Mail Appeal, Year-End Email Solicitation).']
  notes varchar [note: 'Free-text notes and comments about the appeal including lessons learned, special circumstances, and post-campaign observations.']

  pieces_sent varchar [note: 'Total count of solicitation pieces (letters, emails, calls) sent to constituents for this appeal.']
  premium_offered varchar [note: 'Description of any premium or thank-you gift offered to donors responding to this appeal (e.g., tote bag, calendar, recognition pin).']
  response_count varchar [note: 'Total number of constituents who responded to the appeal with a gift or pledge.']
  response_rate_percent decimal(5,2) [note: 'Percentage of constituents who responded to the appeal calculated as (response_count / pieces_sent) * 100.']
  revenue_currency_code decimal(18,2) [note: 'Three-letter ISO 4217 currency code for the total revenue amount (e.g., USD, EUR, GBP).']
  roi_ratio decimal(10,4) [note: 'Return on investment ratio for the appeal calculated as total_revenue_amount / cost_amount. Values greater than 1.0 indicate positive ROI.']
  solicitor_name varchar [note: 'Name of the primary solicitor or fundraiser credited with this appeal (may be staff, volunteer, or board member).']
  start_date date [note: 'Date the appeal becomes active and begins accepting responses.']
  test_segment_flag boolean [note: 'Indicates whether this appeal is part of an A/B test or experimental segment for testing messaging, package, or channel effectiveness.']
  total_revenue_amount decimal(15,2) [note: 'Total gross revenue generated by gifts and pledges attributed to this appeal.']
  created_by varchar [note: 'Username or identifier of the system user who created the appeal record.']

  Note: 'Specific fundraising appeal or solicitation package within a campaign, managed in Raisers Edge NXT. An appeal is a targeted ask sent to a defined segment of constituents via a specific channel (direct mail, email, phone, event). Captures appeal code, appeal name, parent campaign, appeal type (acquisition, renewal, upgrade, lapsed reactivation), mailing date, channel (mail, email, phone, digital), cost, number of pieces sent, response rate, and total revenue generated. Enables granular attribution of gifts to specific solicitation efforts.'
}

  inception_date date [note: 'Date when the fund was established and opened for gift acceptance. Marks the beginning of the fund\'s lifecycle.']
  indirect_cost_rate decimal(5,2) [note: 'Percentage rate for Facilities and Administration (F&A) indirect costs allowed under this fund per donor agreement or NICRA. Expressed as a percentage (e.g., 15.00 for 15%).']
  investment_policy varchar [note: 'Summary of investment strategy and asset allocation policy for endowment or quasi-endowment funds. References board-approved investment policy statement.']
  minimum_gift_amount decimal(15,2) [note: 'Minimum contribution threshold required to designate a gift to this fund. Used for major gift funds, endowments, and named funds with donor-established minimums.']
  modified_by varchar [note: 'Username or identifier of the system user who last modified this fund record. Supports change management and audit compliance.']
  modified_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Timestamp when this fund record was last updated. Supports change tracking and audit compliance.']
  name varchar [note: 'Full descriptive name of the fund as presented to donors and in financial statements. Human-readable identity label for the fund.']
  reporting_frequency varchar [note: 'Required frequency of stewardship and financial reporting to the donor or funding agency for this fund per grant agreement or donor stewardship plan.']
  restriction_description varchar [note: 'Detailed explanation of donor-imposed restrictions, including specific programmatic purposes, geographic limitations, beneficiary populations, or time-bound conditions that govern fund usage.']

  restriction_type varchar [note: 'Nature of donor-imposed restrictions on the fund. Purpose-restricted funds must be used for specific programs or activities; time-restricted funds have temporal constraints; perpetual restrictions apply to endowments.']
  sdg_alignment varchar [note: 'Comma-separated list of United Nations Sustainable Development Goal (SDG) numbers (1-17) to which this fund\'s programmatic purpose aligns. Used for impact reporting and IATI transparency.']

  created_by varchar [note: 'Username or identifier of the system user who created this fund record. Audit trail for accountability and data stewardship.']

  Note: 'SSOT for donor-facing fund records representing restricted/unrestricted gift designations, stewardship tracking, and donor intent. Links to finance.finance_fund for GL-level fund accounting. Distinct from finance.finance_fund which is the SSOT for general ledger fund segments and financial reporting.'
}

  operational_status varchar [note: 'Current operational state of the project site in its lifecycle.']
  pcode varchar [note: 'OCHA-assigned unique place code (P-code) for standardized geographic referencing in humanitarian operations and reporting.']
  population_catchment varchar [note: 'Estimated number of beneficiaries or persons of concern (PoC) within the service catchment area of this project site.']
  postal_code varchar [note: 'Postal or ZIP code for the project site location, if applicable.']
  security_level varchar [note: 'Current security risk assessment level for the project site based on threat analysis and security incidents.']
  site_address varchar [note: 'Physical street address or location description of the project site.']
  site_area_sqm decimal(12,2) [note: 'Total physical area of the project site measured in square meters.']
  site_code varchar [note: 'Externally-known unique alphanumeric code assigned to the project site for field operations and reporting. Used in SitReps and OCHA coordination.']
  site_description varchar [note: 'Detailed narrative description of the project site including physical characteristics, surrounding environment, and operational context.']
  site_manager_name varchar [note: 'Name of the staff member responsible for managing operations at this project site.']
  site_name varchar [note: 'Human-readable name of the project site or operational location.']
  site_type varchar [note: 'Classification of the project site based on its primary function and service delivery model.']
  water_source_available boolean [note: 'Indicates whether a reliable water source is available at or near the project site for WASH operations.']

  Note: 'Represents a physical humanitarian project site (camp, distribution point, health facility, school, WASH point) with OCHA P-code geographic referencing. Supports 3W (Who does What Where) reporting, cluster coordination, and HDX spatial data. Source systems: Kobo Toolbox (GPS), eTools, CCCM site management. Systems-of-record: eTools (field monitoring), InSight, SAP PS. Framework: IATI v2.03 location elements / UN p-codes (OCHA CODs).'
}

Tracking the implementing partner is essential for accountability reporting, partner performance man']

Business justification: Distribution events must be aggregated within MEL reporting periods for donor reporting and results tracking. Pipe-enum overflow candidate with 15+ standard categories.']

  distribution_status varchar [note: 'Current lifecycle status of the distribution event in the operational workflow. [ENUM-REF-CANDIDATE: planned|approved|in_progress|completed|cancelled|suspended|under_review — 7 candidates stripped; promote to reference product]']
  distribution_type varchar [note: 'Classification of the distribution approach: general (universal coverage), targeted (specific beneficiary criteria), blanket (entire population segment), voucher redemption (voucher exchange), emergency response (rapid onset), or seasonal (recurring planned distribution).']

  notes varchar [note: 'Free-text field for additional operational notes, observations, or context about the distribution event.']

  verification_method varchar [note: 'Method used to verify beneficiary identity and eligibility at the distribution point: biometric (fingerprint/iris), token (physical token), ID card (government ID), beneficiary list (pre-registered list), household head (representative), or mobile verification (SMS/app-based).']

  Note: 'Transactional record of a single distribution event (NFI, food, cash, or voucher) conducted at a project site. Captures event date, site, distribution type (general, targeted, blanket, voucher redemption), planned vs. actual beneficiary count, commodity categories, distribution modality (in-kind, cash, voucher, mobile money), responsible cluster, implementing field team, verification method, and PDM scheduling status. Core operational transaction for field service delivery and the primary unit of work for BvA reconciliation against supply pipeline.'
}

Table field.distribution_line {
  distribution_line_id varchar [pk, note: 'Unique identifier for the distribution line item. Primary key.']
  award_id varchar [note: 'Reference to the grant or funding source that financed this distribution line item. Enables grant-level expenditure tracking and compliance reporting.']
  commodity_id varchar [note: 'FK to supply.commodity for the distributed item. Present in MVM stub, carried forward for ECM completeness.']
  distribution_event_id varchar [note: 'Reference to the parent distribution event header. Links this line item to the overall distribution activity.']
  registrant_id varchar [note: 'FK to the beneficiary registrant receiving this distribution line. Present in MVM stub, carried forward for ECM completeness.']
  warehouse_id varchar [note: 'FK to the source warehouse from which goods were dispatched. Present in MVM stub, carried forward for ECM completeness.']
  actual_quantity_distributed decimal(18,3) [note: 'The actual quantity of the item that was distributed to beneficiaries. Used for BvA (Budget versus Actual) reconciliation and MEL reporting.']
  batch_number varchar [note: 'Batch or lot number of the distributed item. Critical for traceability, quality control, and recall management, especially for food, health, and pharmaceutical items.']
  beneficiary_count varchar [note: 'Number of individual beneficiaries or households who received this specific item in this distribution line. Supports disaggregated MEL reporting.']
  cluster_sector varchar [note: 'OCHA humanitarian cluster or sector to which this distribution line item is attributed (e.g., Food Security, WASH, Shelter, Health, Protection).']
  created_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Timestamp when this distribution line record was first created in the system. Supports audit trail and data lineage.']
  cva_transfer_modality varchar [note: 'Transfer modality for this distribution line: cash, voucher, in-kind, hybrid.']
  delivery_confirmation_date date [note: 'Date the beneficiary confirmed receipt. Present in MVM stub, carried forward for ECM completeness.']
  distribution_method varchar [note: 'The method or modality used to distribute this item (e.g., direct hand distribution, voucher redemption, cash transfer for purchase, mobile distribution unit, fixed distribution site).']
  distribution_status varchar [note: 'Current status of this distribution line item within the distribution event lifecycle.']
  donor_earmark varchar [note: 'Identifier or name of the donor whose funds were earmarked for this specific item distribution. Supports restricted fund accounting and donor reporting.']
  expiry_date date [note: 'Expiration or best-before date of the distributed item. Relevant for perishable goods, food, medicines, and time-sensitive NFIs.']
  iati_transaction_type varchar [note: 'IATI-compliant transaction type code for this distribution line, supporting international aid transparency and reporting.']
  item_category varchar [note: 'High-level category of the distributed item. NFI = Non-Food Item, WASH = Water Sanitation and Hygiene.']
  item_code varchar [note: 'Standardized code identifying the specific commodity or NFI item. May align with supply chain master item codes or donor-specific item catalogs.']
  item_description varchar [note: 'Detailed textual description of the distributed item including specifications, brand, quality grade, or other distinguishing characteristics.']
  line_number varchar [note: 'Sequential line number within the distribution event. Used for ordering and referencing specific items within the distribution.']
  modified_by varchar [note: 'Identifier of the user or system that last modified this distribution line record. Supports accountability and audit trail.']
  modified_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Timestamp when this distribution line record was last modified. Supports audit trail and change tracking.']
  mvm_migration_reference varchar [note: 'Reference code linking this record to the legacy MVM distribution_line stub for migration traceability.']
  notes varchar [note: 'Free-text field for additional contextual information, special instructions, or observations related to this distribution line item.']
  pipeline_source varchar [note: 'Identifier of the supply pipeline or procurement source from which the item was drawn (e.g., central warehouse, local procurement, in-kind donation, CERF allocation).']
  planned_quantity decimal(18,3) [note: 'The quantity of the item that was planned or targeted for distribution in this line item, as per the distribution plan or LogFrame target.']
  quality_check_date date [note: 'Date when the quality inspection was performed on this distribution line item.']
  quality_check_status varchar [note: 'Result of quality inspection performed on this item batch before or during distribution. Ensures compliance with SPHERE and CHS standards.']
  rejection_reason varchar [note: 'Reason for partial/full rejection of distributed items. Present in MVM stub, carried forward for ECM completeness.']
  sdg_alignment varchar [note: 'Reference to the UN Sustainable Development Goal(s) that this distribution line item contributes to (e.g., SDG 2: Zero Hunger, SDG 6: Clean Water and Sanitation).']
  substitution_flag boolean [note: 'Whether this line represents a substituted commodity. Present in MVM stub, carried forward for ECM completeness.']
  total_value decimal(18,2) [note: 'The total monetary value of this distribution line (typically unit_value × actual_quantity_distributed). Supports financial reconciliation and grant expenditure tracking.']
  unit_of_measure varchar [note: 'The unit in which the item quantity is measured (e.g., pieces, kilograms, liters, boxes, kits, households).']
  unit_value decimal(18,2) [note: 'The monetary value per unit of the distributed item. Used for financial tracking and donor reporting.']
  variance_quantity decimal(18,3) [note: 'Difference between planned_quantity and actual_quantity_distributed. Positive values indicate over-distribution, negative values indicate under-distribution. Used for BvA analysis.']
  variance_reason varchar [note: 'Explanation for any significant variance between planned and actual quantities. Supports accountability and learning in MEL processes.']
  created_by varchar [note: 'Identifier of the user or system that created this distribution line record. Supports accountability and audit trail.']

  Note: 'ECM-canonical distribution line item entity. Supersedes the MVM-tier stub \'distribution_line\' and is a strict superset of its attributes. Source systems: SAP/ICON procurement, VISION, eTools, WFP LESS/COMET, SCOPE. Tracks individual line items within a distribution event including commodity, quantity, value, and beneficiary counts.'
}

  Note: 'Field assessment capturing multi-sector data collection activities at project sites, including methodology, sample size, key findings, and protection concerns. Sourced from Kobo Toolbox, ODK, or field team submissions.'

Linking assessment_response to data_collection_tool enables data quality tracking by tool version and ']
  household_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to beneficiary.household. Business justification: Household-level assessments link responses to household units. Standard for food security assessments, household economy analysis, and vulnerability assessments. Enables household-level analysis of ne']
  primary_assessment_registrant_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to beneficiary.registrant. Business justification: PDM and assessment surveys collect individual beneficiary responses. Required for beneficiary satisfaction tracking, accountability to affected populations (AAP), and individual-level feedback analysi']
  project_site_id varchar [note: 'Reference to the project site or field location where the assessment was conducted. Links to geographic and operational site master data.']
  children_under_5_count varchar [note: 'Number of children under 5 years of age in the household. Critical for nutrition, health, and protection program targeting.']
  chronic_illness_flag boolean [note: 'Indicator that one or more household members have a chronic illness requiring ongoing medical care. Informs health service delivery planning.']
  consent_data_sharing boolean [note: 'Indicator that the respondent has provided informed consent for their data to be shared with partner organizations for service delivery purposes.']
  consent_follow_up boolean [note: 'Indicator that the respondent has consented to be contacted for follow-up assessments or post-distribution monitoring activities.']

  debt_burden_flag boolean [note: 'Indicator that the household is experiencing significant debt burden affecting their ability to meet basic needs. Used for economic vulnerability profiling.']
  disability_present_flag boolean [note: 'Indicator that one or more household members have a disability. Used for inclusive programming and accessibility considerations.']
  displacement_duration_months varchar [note: 'Number of months the household has been displaced from their place of origin. Indicates protractedness of displacement and informs durable solutions programming.']
  displacement_status varchar [note: 'Classification of the respondent household displacement situation. Critical for targeting humanitarian assistance and understanding population movements.']
  education_access_flag boolean [note: 'Indicator that school-age children in the household have access to education services. Monitors education in emergencies programming effectiveness.']
  elderly_over_60_count varchar [note: 'Number of elderly individuals over 60 years of age in the household. Used for vulnerability assessment and targeted assistance programs.']
  female_count varchar [note: 'Number of female individuals in the household. Enables sex-disaggregated analysis as required by humanitarian accountability standards.']
  food_security_score decimal(5,2) [note: 'Calculated food security index based on household consumption patterns and coping strategies. Higher scores indicate greater food insecurity.']
  gps_accuracy_meters decimal(8,2) [note: 'Accuracy radius of the GPS coordinates in meters. Indicates the precision and reliability of the location data captured during submission.']
  household_size varchar [note: 'Total number of individuals in the respondent household. Key demographic indicator for needs assessment and resource allocation calculations.']
  interview_duration_minutes varchar [note: 'Total time in minutes taken to complete the assessment interview. Used for quality control and enumerator performance monitoring.']
  interview_language varchar [note: 'Language in which the assessment interview was conducted. Important for data quality assessment and ensuring linguistic accessibility.']
  livelihood_status varchar [note: 'Current employment and income generation status of the household. Critical for economic recovery and livelihoods programming.']
  male_count varchar [note: 'Number of male individuals in the household. Enables sex-disaggregated analysis as required by humanitarian accountability standards.']
  modified_timestamp timestamp [note: 'System timestamp when this assessment response record was last modified. Tracks data quality corrections and validation updates.']
  monthly_income_usd decimal(12,2) [note: 'Estimated total monthly household income in United States Dollars. Used for economic vulnerability assessment and cash transfer program targeting.']
  primary_need_category varchar [note: 'The most urgent need identified by the respondent household. Used for prioritization and resource allocation in humanitarian response. [ENUM-REF-CANDIDATE: food|water|shelter|health|protection|education|nfi|wash|livelihoods|psychosocial — promote to reference product]']
  protection_concern_flag boolean [note: 'Indicator that the household has reported protection concerns requiring specialized follow-up. Triggers referral to protection case management services.']
  protection_concern_type varchar [note: 'Classification of the protection concern reported. Highly sensitive field requiring strict confidentiality and ethical data handling protocols.']
  referral_required_flag boolean [note: 'Indicator that the assessment identified needs requiring referral to specialized services such as protection, health, or case management.']
  response_status varchar [note: 'Current lifecycle status of the assessment response indicating data quality validation state and approval workflow position.']
  shelter_condition varchar [note: 'Assessment of the physical condition and habitability of the household shelter. Used for prioritizing shelter repair and upgrade interventions.']
  shelter_type varchar [note: 'Classification of the household current shelter arrangement. Critical for shelter and NFI (Non-Food Item) programming and vulnerability assessment.']
  submission_latitude decimal(10,7) [note: 'GPS latitude coordinate captured at the time and location of survey submission. Used for spatial analysis and verification of field coverage.']
  submission_longitude decimal(10,7) [note: 'GPS longitude coordinate captured at the time and location of survey submission. Used for spatial analysis and verification of field coverage.']
  submission_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Date and time when the assessment response was submitted by the enumerator. Represents the principal business event time for this transaction.']
  validation_notes varchar [note: 'Free-text notes from data validation and quality assurance review. Documents any data quality issues, corrections, or contextual information.']
  water_access_minutes varchar [note: 'Time in minutes required for household members to access safe drinking water. Key WASH (Water Sanitation and Hygiene) indicator for service delivery monitoring.']

  Note: 'Individual survey or interview response record collected during a field assessment, sourced from KoboToolbox or CommCare mobile data collection. Captures respondent anonymized ID, assessment form ID, submission timestamp, GPS coordinates of submission, enumerator ID, response status (submitted, validated, rejected), and key structured response fields (household size, displacement status, primary needs, protection concerns). Enables disaggregated analysis by sex, age, IDP status, and vulnerability category.'
}

  emergency_id varchar [note: 'Reference to the crisis or emergency context this situation report addresses.']

Business justification: Situation reports document operational progress and constraints for specific program interventions. Donors and cluster leads require sitreps to show intervention-level implementation status, beneficia']

Business justification: Situation reports often consolidate partner-implemented activities for cluster coordination and donor reporting. Linking enables tracking partner contributions to overall response, supports 3W reporti']

Linking sitrep to reporting_period enables alignment of situation report publication with the MEL calendar — a standard NGO donor reporting and ']
  team_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to field.field_team. Business justification: Situation Reports (SitReps) are authored and submitted by field teams. The field_team table has a last_sitrep_date attribute confirming this operational relationship. Adding field_team_id to sitrep en']

  health_consultations_completed varchar [note: 'Number of health consultations or medical encounters completed during the reporting period.']
  hrp_progress_percentage decimal(5,2) [note: 'Percentage of Humanitarian Response Plan targets achieved as of the end of the reporting period.']
  last_modified_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Timestamp when this situation report record was last modified.']
  nfi_distributions_completed varchar [note: 'Number of non-food item distribution events completed during the reporting period.']
  nfi_kits_distributed varchar [note: 'Total number of NFI kits or packages distributed during the reporting period.']
  ocha_submission_required boolean [note: 'Indicates whether this situation report is required to be submitted to OCHA for coordination purposes.']
  operational_constraints varchar [note: 'Narrative description of operational constraints, access issues, security incidents, or logistical challenges encountered during the reporting period.']
  protection_cases_registered varchar [note: 'Number of new protection cases registered during the reporting period, including GBV, child protection, and other protection concerns.']
  publication_date date [note: 'Date when the situation report was officially published or released.']
  report_number varchar [note: 'Externally-known unique identifier for this situation report, typically following organizational numbering convention.']
  report_status varchar [note: 'Current lifecycle status of the situation report (draft, under review, approved, submitted to OCHA/donors, published, archived).']
  report_title varchar [note: 'Descriptive title of the situation report, typically including crisis name and reporting period.']
  reporting_frequency varchar [note: 'Frequency at which this situation report is issued (daily, weekly, bi-weekly, monthly, ad-hoc for special events, flash for immediate crisis updates).']
  reporting_period_end_date date [note: 'End date of the period covered by this situation report.']
  reporting_period_start_date date [note: 'Start date of the period covered by this situation report.']
  resource_gaps_summary varchar [note: 'Narrative description of critical resource gaps, funding shortfalls, or supply needs identified during the reporting period.']
  security_incidents_count varchar [note: 'Number of security incidents affecting staff, operations, or beneficiaries during the reporting period.']
  shelter_units_provided varchar [note: 'Number of shelter units (tents, transitional shelters, housing units) provided during the reporting period.']
  submission_date date [note: 'Date when the situation report was submitted to OCHA, donors, or coordination bodies.']
  total_beneficiaries_reached varchar [note: 'Total number of unique beneficiaries reached across all sectors during the reporting period.']
  wash_beneficiaries_reached varchar [note: 'Number of beneficiaries reached through WASH interventions during the reporting period.']

  Note: 'Situation Report (SitRep) following OCHA reporting standards for humanitarian operations. Captures cluster-level response metrics, beneficiary reach, funding gaps, and operational constraints. Feeds into HRP progress tracking and donor reporting. Source systems: eTools, OCHA reporting templates, ReliefWeb.'
}

Table field.team {
  team_id varchar [pk, note: 'Unique identifier for the field team or mobile unit. Primary key.']
  country_office_id varchar [note: 'Reference to the country office or field office to which this team is administratively assigned.']
  emergency_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to field.emergency. Business justification: Field teams are deployed specifically in response to declared humanitarian emergencies. The field_team table has deployment_start_date and deployment_end_date attributes that align with emergency resp']

  partner_org_id varchar [note: 'Reference to a partner organization (CBO, CSO, INGO) if this team operates under a partnership or consortium arrangement.']
  project_site_id varchar [note: 'Reference to the project site or field office serving as the team\'s operational base or home location.']

[ENUM-REF-CANDIDATE: health|nutrition|wash|shelter|protection|education|logistics|food_security|early_recovery|camp_coordination|emergency_telecommunications — 11 candidates stripped; promote to reference product]']
  code varchar [note: 'Short alphanumeric code used for operational identification and reporting (e.g., \'DT-001\', \'MHU-03\', \'WASH-A\').']
  communication_equipment varchar [note: 'Description of communication equipment assigned to the team (e.g., satellite phones, radios, mobile devices, GPS units) to maintain contact with coordination centers and ensure safety.']
  coverage_area_description varchar [note: 'Textual description of the geographic area or communities covered by this team (e.g., \'Northern District villages\', \'IDP (Internally Displaced Person) camps in Region 3\').']
  created_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Timestamp when this team record was first created in the system.']
  deployment_end_date date [note: 'Planned or actual date when the team deployment concluded or is scheduled to conclude. Null for ongoing deployments.']
  deployment_start_date date [note: 'Date when the team was first deployed or became operational.']
