  last_modified_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Timestamp when this team record was most recently updated.']
  last_performance_review_date date [note: 'Date of the most recent formal performance review or evaluation conducted for this team.']
  last_sitrep_date date [note: 'Date when the team last submitted a SitRep (Situation Report) to the coordination center or country office.']
  mobile_data_collection_platform varchar [note: 'Name of the mobile data collection tool or platform used by this team (e.g., KoboToolbox, CommCare, ODK, DHIS2 mobile).']
  monthly_operational_budget decimal(15,2) [note: 'Planned monthly operational budget allocated for this team\'s activities, including salaries, transport, supplies, and per diems.']
  name varchar [note: 'Human-readable name or designation of the field team (e.g., \'Delta Distribution Team\', \'Mobile Health Unit 3\', \'WASH Response Team Alpha\').']
  notes varchar [note: 'Free-text field for additional operational notes, special considerations, or context about the team\'s deployment and activities.']
  operational_status varchar [note: 'Current operational state of the team. Active: currently deployed and operational; Standby: ready for deployment; Deployed: in field assignment; Resting: between deployments; Disbanded: team dissolved; Suspended: temporarily inactive.']
  performance_rating varchar [note: 'Most recent performance assessment rating for the team based on MEL (Monitoring Evaluation and Learning) indicators, service delivery quality, and beneficiary feedback.']
  primary_language varchar [note: 'ISO 639-3 three-letter code for the primary language spoken by the team for beneficiary communication (e.g., ENG for English, ARA for Arabic, FRA for French).']
  safety_clearance_level varchar [note: 'Security risk level classification for the team\'s operational area, aligned with UN security phase system. Determines safety protocols and movement restrictions.']
  size varchar [note: 'Total number of staff and volunteers currently assigned to this team.']
  team_type varchar [note: 'Classification of the team based on its primary operational function. Distribution teams handle NFI (Non-Food Item) distributions; CHW (Community Health Worker) teams provide mobile health outreach; WASH (Water Sanitation and Hygiene) teams deliver water and sanitation interventions; assessment teams conduct FGD (Focus Group Discussion) and KII (Key Informant Interview); protection teams address GBV (Gender-Based Violence) and child protection; rapid response teams handle emergency deployments; nutrition teams manage SAM (Severe Acute Malnutrition) and GAM (Global Acute Malnutrition) programs; education teams deliver learning programs; shelter teams provide housing assistance; logistics teams manage supply chain and transport. [ENUM-REF-CANDIDATE: distribution|community_health_worker|wash|assessment|protection|rapid_response|nutrition|education|shelter|logistics — 10 candidates stripped; promote to reference product]']
  training_certifications varchar [note: 'Comma-separated list of key training certifications held by team members (e.g., \'SPHERE\', \'CHS\', \'PSS (Psychosocial Support)\', \'GBV Response\', \'First Aid\').']

  Note: 'SSOT for field operations teams deployed to project sites for humanitarian response, including staff-led teams with security clearances, equipment, and operational budgets. Distinct from volunteer.volunteer_team which manages volunteer-led community teams.'
}

Table field.security_incident {
  security_incident_id varchar [pk, note: 'Unique identifier for the security incident record. Primary key.']

  country_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to field.country. Business justification: security_incident has country_code (STRING) but no FK to the country reference table. Security incidents are country-specific events requiring linkage to authoritative country data including security_']
  country_office_id varchar [note: 'Reference to the country office under whose jurisdiction the security incident occurred.']
  emergency_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to field.emergency. Business justification: Security incidents occur within the operational context of declared humanitarian emergencies. Linking security_incident to emergency enables emergency-level security analysis, supports UNDSS Security ']
  intervention_id varchar [note: 'Reference to the program or project under which the affected staff or activities were operating at the time of the security incident, if applicable.']
  partner_org_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to partnership.partner_org. Business justification: Security incidents involving partner staff or assets must be tracked by partner organization for duty of care obligations, insurance claims, risk register updates, and partner capacity assessment. Cri']
  project_site_id varchar [note: 'Reference to the specific project site or field location where the security incident occurred, if applicable.']
  team_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to field.field_team. Business justification: Security incidents directly affect field teams deployed in the area. The security_incident table tracks affected_personnel_count and staff_fatality_count which are field team personnel metrics. Linkin']
  admin_level_1 varchar [note: 'First-level administrative division (e.g., state, province, region) where the incident occurred.']
  admin_level_2 varchar [note: 'Second-level administrative division (e.g., district, county) where the incident occurred.']

  asset_damage_description varchar [note: 'Narrative description of damage to organizational assets (vehicles, equipment, facilities, supplies) resulting from the security incident.']
  case_closed_date date [note: 'Date when the security incident case was formally closed after investigation and resolution (yyyy-MM-dd format).']
  corrective_actions_taken varchar [note: 'Description of corrective or preventive actions implemented following the security incident to mitigate future risk (e.g., enhanced security protocols, staff training, route changes).']
  created_timestamp timestamp [note: 'System timestamp when the security incident record was first created in the database (yyyy-MM-ddTHH:mm:ss.SSSXXX format).']

  estimated_asset_loss_usd decimal(15,2) [note: 'Estimated monetary value of asset damage or loss in United States Dollars (USD) resulting from the security incident.']

  incident_date date [note: 'Date when the security incident occurred in the field (yyyy-MM-dd format).']
  incident_description varchar [note: 'Detailed narrative description of the security incident, including sequence of events, circumstances, and context.']
  incident_location_name varchar [note: 'Descriptive name or address of the specific location where the security incident occurred (e.g., village name, road name, facility name).']
  incident_number varchar [note: 'Externally-known unique incident reference number assigned by security management system, following format SI-YYYY-NNNNNN.']
  incident_severity varchar [note: 'Severity classification of the security incident based on impact to personnel, assets, and operations. Critical = life-threatening or major asset loss; Major = serious injury or significant damage; Moderate = minor injury or moderate damage; Minor = no injury, minimal damage.']
  incident_status varchar [note: 'Current lifecycle status of the security incident case in the investigation and resolution workflow.']
  incident_time timestamp [note: 'Precise date and time when the security incident occurred, in local time zone (yyyy-MM-ddTHH:mm:ss.SSSXXX format).']
  incident_type varchar [note: 'Classification of the security incident by nature of threat or event. [ENUM-REF-CANDIDATE: armed_robbery|carjacking|kidnapping|staff_detention|explosive_hazard|civil_unrest|armed_conflict|theft|burglary|assault|sexual_violence|harassment|vehicle_accident|fire|natural_disaster|health_emergency|other — promote to reference product]']
  inso_report_date date [note: 'Date when the security incident was reported to INSO, if applicable (yyyy-MM-dd format).']
  investigation_findings varchar [note: 'Summary of findings from the security incident investigation, including root causes, contributing factors, and lessons learned.']
  investigation_status varchar [note: 'Current status of the internal or external investigation into the security incident.']
  latitude decimal(10,7) [note: 'Geographic latitude coordinate of the incident location in decimal degrees (WGS84 datum).']
  local_authorities_report_date date [note: 'Date when the security incident was reported to local authorities, if applicable (yyyy-MM-dd format).']
  longitude decimal(10,7) [note: 'Geographic longitude coordinate of the incident location in decimal degrees (WGS84 datum).']
  modified_timestamp timestamp [note: 'System timestamp when the security incident record was last modified or updated (yyyy-MM-ddTHH:mm:ss.SSSXXX format).']
  pcode varchar [note: 'OCHA-standardized place code (P-Code) uniquely identifying the administrative location of the incident for humanitarian coordination.']
  police_case_number varchar [note: 'Official case or reference number assigned by local law enforcement authorities for the security incident, if reported.']
  reported_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Date and time when the security incident was first reported to the organization\'s security management system (yyyy-MM-ddTHH:mm:ss.SSSXXX format).']
  reported_to_inso boolean [note: 'Indicates whether the security incident was formally reported to the International NGO Safety Organisation (INSO) for coordination and information sharing among NGOs.']
  reported_to_local_authorities boolean [note: 'Indicates whether the security incident was reported to local law enforcement or government authorities.']
  reported_to_undss boolean [note: 'Indicates whether the security incident was formally reported to the United Nations Department of Safety and Security (UNDSS) for coordination and situational awareness.']
  sitrep_included boolean [note: 'Indicates whether the security incident was included in a formal Situation Report (SitRep) distributed to stakeholders, donors, or coordination bodies.']
  staff_fatality_count varchar [note: 'Number of staff members who died as a result of the security incident.']
  staff_injured_count varchar [note: 'Number of staff members who sustained physical injuries as a result of the security incident.']
  undss_report_date date [note: 'Date when the security incident was reported to UNDSS, if applicable (yyyy-MM-dd format).']

  Note: 'Records security incidents affecting humanitarian operations per UNDSS Security Level System (SLS) and INSO reporting standards. Tracks staff safety events, asset losses, and coordination with UNDSS/INSO. Supports Aid Worker Security Database (AWSD) reporting. Source systems: UNDSS SSIRS, INSO, internal security management.'
}

Table field.country {
  country_id varchar [pk, note: 'Primary key for country']

  capital_city varchar [note: 'Name of the capital city of the country. Often the location of the country office headquarters.']
  cluster_coordination_active boolean [note: 'Indicates whether the OCHA cluster coordination system is active in this country. Affects reporting obligations and inter-agency coordination requirements.']
  code varchar [note: 'Standardized code representing the country classification or category.']
  code_alpha2 varchar [note: 'Two-letter country code as defined by ISO 3166-1 alpha-2 standard. Used for international identification and data exchange.']

  emergency_declared_flag boolean [note: 'Boolean flag indicating whether the emergency declared condition applies.']

  humanitarian_context_flag boolean [note: 'Boolean flag indicating whether the humanitarian context condition applies.']

  income_classification varchar [note: 'World Bank income classification category for the country. Used for program eligibility, funding allocation, and needs assessment.']

  last_updated_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Timestamp when this country record was last modified. Used for audit trail and change tracking.']

  modified_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Date and time when the modified event occurred for this country.']
  mou_expiry_date date [note: 'Expiry date of the current Memorandum of Understanding with the national government. Null if no MOU exists or if MOU is indefinite.']
  name varchar [note: 'Official full name of the country as recognized by the United Nations.']
  notes varchar [note: 'Free-text field for additional operational notes, context, or special considerations for this country. Used for knowledge management and operational guidance.']
  ocha_presence boolean [note: 'Indicates whether OCHA has an active presence in the country. Determines cluster coordination mechanisms and humanitarian reporting requirements.']
  office_established boolean [note: 'Indicates whether the organization has an established country office with permanent presence in this country.']
  office_establishment_date date [note: 'Date when the country office was officially established. Null if no country office exists.']
  official_language varchar [note: 'Attribute capturing the official language information for the country entity.']
  official_languages varchar [note: 'Comma-separated list of official languages spoken in the country. Used for translation requirements, field staff recruitment, and beneficiary communication planning.']
  operational_status varchar [note: 'Current operational status of the country for field operations. Indicates whether the organization has active programs, suspended operations, or restricted access.']
  population varchar [note: 'Total population of the country based on the most recent census or UN estimate. Used for needs assessment and program scale planning.']
  population_year varchar [note: 'Year of the population estimate or census data. Indicates the currency of population data.']
  primary_donor_countries varchar [note: 'Comma-separated list of primary donor countries or institutions funding operations in this country. Used for donor reporting and relationship management.']
  region varchar [note: 'Geographic region classification for the country. Used for regional program coordination and reporting aggregation.']
  region_name varchar [note: 'Human-readable name or label for the region.']
  security_risk_level varchar [note: 'Current security risk assessment level for the country. Determines staff travel restrictions, security protocols, and operational modalities.']
  small_island_developing_state boolean [note: 'Indicates whether the country is classified as a Small Island Developing State. Relevant for climate adaptation and disaster risk reduction programs.']
  sub_region varchar [note: 'Geographic sub-region classification providing more granular regional grouping for operational planning and analysis.']
  sub_region_name varchar [note: 'Human-readable name or label for the sub region.']
  tax_exemption_status varchar [note: 'Tax exemption status granted to the organization by the national government. Affects financial planning and procurement.']
  un_member_state_flag boolean [note: 'Boolean flag indicating whether the un member state condition applies.']
  visa_requirements varchar [note: 'Description of visa requirements and procedures for international staff deployment to this country. Used for staff travel planning and compliance.']

  Note: 'Master reference table for country. Referenced by country_id.'
}

Table field.emergency {
  emergency_id varchar [pk, note: 'Primary key for emergency']
  country_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to field.country. Business justification: emergency has primary_country_code (STRING) but no FK to the country reference table. The country table is the authoritative source for country data. Adding country_id FK normalizes the country refere']
  country_office_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to field.country_office. Business justification: Humanitarian emergencies are managed and coordinated by country offices. The emergency table already has country_id but lacks a direct link to the country_office responsible for the emergency response']

  cerf_allocation_flag boolean [note: 'Boolean flag indicating whether the cerf allocation condition applies.']
  cerf_allocation_received boolean [note: 'Indicates whether the organization received funding from the UN Central Emergency Response Fund for this emergency.']
  closure_date date [note: 'Date when the emergency was officially closed and all operations ceased.']

  code varchar [note: 'Externally-known unique business identifier for the emergency, following organizational coding standards (e.g., SYR-2023-EQKE01).']
  created_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Timestamp when this emergency record was first created in the system.']
  declaration_date date [note: 'Date when the emergency was officially declared by the organization or governing authority.']
  description varchar [note: 'Detailed narrative description of the emergency context, impact, and response strategy.']
  disaster_category varchar [note: 'Specific disaster category providing granular classification of the emergency event type.']
  displaced_population_count varchar [note: 'Estimated number of individuals displaced or forced to leave their homes due to the emergency.']
  emergency_status varchar [note: 'Current lifecycle status of the emergency response operation.']
  emergency_type varchar [note: 'Classification of the emergency by its primary cause or nature.']
  fgd_conducted_count varchar [note: 'Number of Focus Group Discussions conducted as part of field assessments for this emergency.']
  flash_appeal_issued boolean [note: 'Indicates whether an OCHA Flash Appeal was issued for this emergency to mobilize rapid funding.']
  funding_received_usd decimal(18,2) [note: 'Total funding received in US Dollars for the emergency response as of the last update.']

  is_active boolean [note: 'Indicates whether this emergency record is currently active and operational in the system.']
  kii_conducted_count varchar [note: 'Number of Key Informant Interviews conducted as part of field assessments for this emergency.']
  kobo_survey_deployed boolean [note: 'Indicates whether mobile data collection surveys via KoboToolbox were deployed for field assessments in this emergency.']
  last_modified_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Timestamp when this emergency record was last updated in the system.']
  lead_clusters varchar [note: 'Comma-separated list of humanitarian clusters where the organization holds lead or co-lead responsibility (e.g., \'WASH, Shelter, Protection\').']
  lessons_learned varchar [note: 'Summary of key lessons learned and best practices documented during or after the emergency response.']
  mobile_health_outreach_planned boolean [note: 'Indicates whether mobile health clinics or outreach services are planned as part of the health response.']
  modified_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Date and time when the modified event occurred for this emergency.']
  name varchar [note: 'Human-readable name or title of the emergency event (e.g., \'Syria Earthquake Response 2023\', \'Bangladesh Flood Relief\').']
  nfi_distribution_planned boolean [note: 'Indicates whether distribution of non-food items (shelter materials, hygiene kits, household items) is planned.']
  ocha_appeal_reference varchar [note: 'Attribute capturing the ocha appeal reference information for the emergency entity.']
  onset_date date [note: 'Date when the emergency event first occurred or began impacting the affected population.']
  people_in_need_count varchar [note: 'Count or number of people in need items associated with this record.']
  people_targeted_count varchar [note: 'Count or number of people targeted items associated with this record.']
  protection_concerns_identified varchar [note: 'Summary of key protection concerns identified during assessments (e.g., \'GBV risk, child protection, housing land property rights\').']
  rapid_assessment_completed boolean [note: 'Indicates whether a rapid needs assessment was completed within the first 72 hours of the emergency.']
  response_end_date date [note: 'Date when the emergency response operations were concluded or transitioned to recovery phase.']
  response_modality varchar [note: 'Description of the primary response delivery mechanisms employed (e.g., \'In-kind distribution, Cash and Voucher Assistance (CVA), Service delivery\').']
  response_start_date date [note: 'Date when the organization initiated its emergency response operations.']
  severity_level varchar [note: 'IASC system-wide emergency classification level indicating scale and required response capacity (Level 1: country-led, Level 2: UN coordination, Level 3: system-wide mobilization).']
  sitrep_frequency varchar [note: 'Frequency at which situation reports are generated and disseminated for this emergency.']
  targeted_beneficiaries_count varchar [note: 'Number of beneficiaries the organization plans to reach through its emergency response interventions.']
  wash_interventions_planned boolean [note: 'Indicates whether WASH sector interventions are included in the emergency response plan.']

  Note: 'Represents a declared humanitarian emergency (L1/L2/L3 or IASC System-Wide Scale-Up) triggering coordinated response under OCHA. Tracks HRP/Flash Appeal issuance, CERF allocations, cluster activations, and response modalities. Source systems: OCHA FTS, ReliefWeb, Humanitarian InSight, GDACS.'
}

  unit_of_measure varchar [note: 'Unit in which the indicator is measured. Examples include count, percentage, ratio, households, beneficiaries, or Mid-Upper Arm Circumference (MUAC) measurements.']
  version varchar [note: 'Version identifier for the logical framework. LogFrames are often revised during program amendments or mid-term reviews.']

  Note: 'Logical framework (logframe) linking interventions to results chains (goal, purpose, outputs, activities) with objectively verifiable indicators, means of verification, and assumptions. Aligned to DAC evaluation criteria.'

  responsible_unit varchar [note: 'The organizational unit, department, or team responsible for addressing or implementing the recommendation.']
  risk_implication varchar [note: 'Description of risks or potential negative consequences if the finding or recommendation is not addressed.']
  sdg_alignment varchar [note: 'Sustainable Development Goal(s) that this finding relates to or impacts (e.g., SDG 1, SDG 3, SDG 5).']
  sector varchar [note: 'Humanitarian or development sector this finding relates to (e.g., WASH, health, education, protection, food security).']
  target_completion_date date [note: 'Planned or agreed date by which the recommendation should be implemented or the issue addressed.']

  Note: 'Individual finding from an evaluation with associated recommendations, management response, and implementation tracking.'
}

  partner_org_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to partnership.partner_org. Business justification: Tools deployed by/for partners in sub-award contexts. Business need: partner data collection coordination, tool standardization across partners, data quality protocol enforcement.']
  program_logframe_id varchar [note: 'Identifier of the LogFrame (Logical Framework) to which this tool is aligned.']

Linking data_collection_tool to project_site enables geographic tracking of tool deployment and site-level data collection planning — a co']
  reporting_period_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to mel.reporting_period. The data_collection_tool table has `deployment_start_']
  safeguarding_risk_assessment_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to safeguarding.risk_assessment. Business justification: Data collection tools (surveys, FGDs, KIIs) involving beneficiaries require safeguarding risk assessments to identify risks (power dynamics, retraumatization, child safeguarding) and embed mitigation.']
  team_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to field.field_team. Business justification: Data collection tools are assigned to and operated by field teams. Linking data_collection_tool to field_team enables tracking tool-team assignments and supports field data quality management — a stan']

  data_quality_validation_rules varchar [note: 'Description or reference to validation rules embedded in the tool to ensure data quality at point of collection.']
  deployment_end_date date [note: 'Date on which the data collection tool was retired or deactivated from field use.']

  estimated_completion_time_minutes varchar [note: 'Expected time in minutes required to complete the data collection tool.']

  last_modified_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Timestamp when this data collection tool record was last updated or modified.']
  notes varchar [note: 'Additional free-text notes or comments about the data collection tool.']
  primary_language varchar [note: 'ISO 639 language code for the primary language of the data collection tool.']

  tool_code varchar [note: 'Unique business identifier code for the data collection tool, used for external reference and integration.']

  tool_name varchar [note: 'Human-readable name of the data collection tool or instrument.']
  tool_status varchar [note: 'Current lifecycle status of the data collection tool.']
  tool_type varchar [note: 'Classification of the data collection tool by format and platform.']
  version_number varchar [note: 'Version identifier of the data collection tool following semantic versioning convention.']

  Note: 'Definition of a data collection instrument (survey, form, checklist) deployed via Kobo Toolbox, ODK, or other platforms. Includes versioning, language support, and ethical review status.'
}

Table mel.reporting_period {
  reporting_period_id varchar [pk, note: 'Primary key for reporting_period']
  mel_logframe_id varchar [note: 'Reference to the specific version of the Logical Framework (LogFrame) that is active during this reporting period. Ensures indicator targets and actuals are measured against the correct framework version.']
  parent_reporting_period_id varchar [note: 'Reference to a parent reporting period for hierarchical period structures (e.g., monthly periods rolling up to quarterly periods, quarterly to annual). Null for top-level periods.']
  primary_prior_reporting_period_id varchar [note: 'Self-referencing FK on reporting_period (prior_reporting_period_id)']
  baseline_period_flag boolean [note: 'Indicates whether this reporting period represents a baseline measurement period for program indicators (True) or not (False). Baseline periods establish the starting point for impact measurement.']
  calendar_year varchar [note: 'The calendar year in which the reporting period falls, used for temporal analysis and trend reporting.']
  closed_date date [note: 'The date on which the reporting period was officially closed and locked for further data entry. Null if the period is still open or in draft status.']
  created_timestamp timestamp [note: 'The timestamp when this reporting period record was first created in the system. Used for audit trail and data lineage tracking.']
  dac_criteria_focus varchar [note: 'The primary DAC evaluation criteria focus for this period (e.g., \'Relevance\', \'Effectiveness\', \'Efficiency\', \'Impact\', \'Sustainability\', \'Coherence\'). Multiple criteria may be listed as comma-separated values.']
  data_collection_deadline date [note: 'The deadline date by which all data collection activities for this reporting period must be completed. Used for MEL workflow management.']
  data_quality_audit_flag boolean [note: 'Indicates whether a data quality audit or verification exercise is scheduled for this reporting period (True) or not (False). Ensures data integrity and reliability.']
  dhis2_period_code varchar [note: 'The period identifier used in DHIS2 system for aggregate health data reporting. Enables integration with DHIS2 for health program monitoring.']
  donor_reporting_cycle varchar [note: 'The specific donor reporting cycle or phase that this period supports (e.g., \'Year 1 Q2\', \'Mid-term Review\', \'Final Report\'). Used to align with donor contractual obligations.']
  duration_days varchar [note: 'The total number of calendar days covered by this reporting period, calculated from start_date to end_date inclusive.']
  end_date date [note: 'The last date of the reporting period when data collection and monitoring activities conclude.']
  endline_period_flag boolean [note: 'Indicates whether this reporting period represents an endline measurement period for program indicators (True) or not (False). Endline periods capture final outcomes and impact.']
  evaluation_type varchar [note: 'The type of evaluation activity conducted during this reporting period, aligned with DAC evaluation criteria (formative, process, outcome, impact, ex-post, real-time). Null if no formal evaluation is scheduled.']
  fgd_scheduled_flag boolean [note: 'Indicates whether Focus Group Discussions (FGDs) are scheduled to be conducted during this reporting period (True) or not (False). Used for qualitative data collection planning.']
  fiscal_year varchar [note: 'The fiscal year to which this reporting period belongs (e.g., FY2024, FY2023-24). Used for budget alignment and annual donor reporting.']
  is_active boolean [note: 'Indicates whether this reporting period record is currently active and available for use (True) or has been soft-deleted/deactivated (False). Used for logical deletion without physical removal.']
  kii_scheduled_flag boolean [note: 'Indicates whether Key Informant Interviews (KIIs) are scheduled to be conducted during this reporting period (True) or not (False). Used for qualitative data collection planning.']
  midline_period_flag boolean [note: 'Indicates whether this reporting period represents a midline measurement period for program indicators (True) or not (False). Midline periods assess progress at the program midpoint.']
  modified_timestamp timestamp [note: 'The timestamp when this reporting period record was last modified. Used for audit trail and change tracking.']
  month_number varchar [note: 'The month number (1-12) within the year, applicable for monthly reporting periods. Null for non-monthly periods.']
  notes varchar [note: 'Free-text field for additional notes, comments, or contextual information about the reporting period, including any anomalies, challenges, or special circumstances affecting data collection.']
  pdm_cycle_flag boolean [note: 'Indicates whether this reporting period includes a Post-Distribution Monitoring (PDM) cycle for assessing the effectiveness and impact of distributed aid (True) or not (False).']
  period_code varchar [note: 'Business identifier code for the reporting period, used in external communications and donor reports (e.g., Q1-2024, FY2024, JAN2024).']
  period_description varchar [note: 'Detailed description of the reporting period, including any special characteristics, contextual information, or notes about data collection activities during this period.']
  period_name varchar [note: 'Human-readable name or label for the reporting period (e.g., \'First Quarter 2024\', \'January 2024\', \'Fiscal Year 2024\').']
  period_status varchar [note: 'Current lifecycle status of the reporting period (draft: not yet started, active: currently open for data collection, closed: finalized and locked, archived: historical record).']
  period_type varchar [note: 'Classification of the reporting period by its duration or frequency (daily, weekly, monthly, quarterly, semi-annual, annual, or custom).']
  quarter_number varchar [note: 'The quarter number (1-4) within the fiscal or calendar year, applicable for quarterly reporting periods. Null for non-quarterly periods.']
  report_submission_deadline date [note: 'The deadline date by which the final report for this period must be submitted to donors or stakeholders. Critical for compliance with donor agreements.']
  reporting_frequency varchar [note: 'The frequency at which data is expected to be reported during this period, aligned with donor requirements and MEL framework schedules.']
  start_date date [note: 'The first date of the reporting period when data collection and monitoring activities begin.']
  week_number varchar [note: 'The ISO week number (1-53) within the year, applicable for weekly reporting periods. Null for non-weekly periods.']

  Note: 'Time period definition for MEL reporting cycles (monthly, quarterly, annual) with DHIS2 period codes and donor reporting alignment.'
}

Table supply.commodity {
  commodity_id varchar [pk, note: 'Unique identifier for the humanitarian commodity or relief item in the supply chain master catalog.']
  category varchar [note: 'Primary classification of the commodity by humanitarian sector or program area (e.g., shelter, health, nutrition, WASH, food, education, protection, NFI general, emergency relief). [ENUM-REF-CANDIDATE: shelter|health|nutrition|wash|food|education|protection|nfi_general|emergency_relief|other — 10 candidates stripped; promote to reference product]']
  code varchar [note: 'Standardized alphanumeric code uniquely identifying the commodity item across procurement, warehousing, and distribution systems. Often aligned with UNSPSC or internal cataloging standards.']
  cold_chain_required_flag boolean [note: 'Indicates whether the commodity requires continuous temperature-controlled storage and transport (cold chain) from procurement through distribution, typical for vaccines and certain medical supplies.']
  commodity_status varchar [note: 'Current lifecycle status of the commodity in the master catalog (e.g., active for procurement and distribution, inactive, discontinued, pending approval, restricted use).']
  country_of_origin varchar [note: 'Three-letter ISO country code indicating the country where the commodity is manufactured or produced, relevant for customs, import regulations, and donor reporting.']
  created_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Date and time when the commodity record was first created in the master catalog system, following organizational timestamp format.']
  description varchar [note: 'Detailed textual description of the commodity including specifications, intended use, and any special characteristics relevant to field operations and beneficiary distribution.']
  diluent_required_flag boolean [note: 'Whether a diluent is required for reconstitution.']
  donor_restricted_flag boolean [note: 'Indicates whether the commodity is subject to donor-imposed restrictions on use, distribution geography, or beneficiary eligibility, requiring compliance tracking in distribution planning.']
  donor_restriction_notes varchar [note: 'Free-text description of specific donor restrictions or conditions attached to the commodity, including geographic limitations, beneficiary eligibility criteria, or usage constraints.']
  doses_per_vial varchar [note: 'Number of doses per vial for vaccines.']
  effective_end_date date [note: 'Date after which the commodity record is no longer active for procurement and distribution, supporting lifecycle management and historical tracking. Null indicates open-ended validity.']
  effective_start_date date [note: 'Date from which the commodity record becomes active and available for procurement and distribution operations, supporting temporal validity tracking.']
  epi_schedule_inclusion_flag boolean [note: 'Whether this vaccine is part of the national Expanded Programme on Immunization schedule.']
  gavi_cofinanced_flag boolean [note: 'Boolean flag indicating whether the gavi cofinanced condition applies.']
  gavi_eligible_flag boolean [note: 'Whether this commodity is eligible for Gavi co-financing']
  harmonized_tariff_code varchar [note: 'International Harmonized System (HS) tariff classification code for the commodity, used for customs clearance, import/export documentation, and duty calculation.']
  hazard_classification varchar [note: 'Specific hazard classification code or category (e.g., flammable, corrosive, toxic, biohazard) if the commodity is flagged as hazardous material, following UN or GHS standards.']
  hazardous_material_flag boolean [note: 'Indicates whether the commodity is classified as hazardous material requiring special handling, storage, and transport procedures per international safety standards.']
  in_kind_donation_eligible_flag boolean [note: 'Indicates whether the commodity is eligible to be received as an in-kind donation from corporate or individual donors, subject to organizational acceptance policies and quality standards.']
  is_vaccine_flag boolean [note: 'Whether this commodity is a vaccine requiring cold chain management.']
  kit_assembly_flag boolean [note: 'Indicates whether the commodity is a pre-assembled kit or bundle composed of multiple component items (e.g., hygiene kit, shelter kit, medical kit) rather than a single item.']
  kit_component_count varchar [note: 'Number of distinct component items included in the kit or assembly if the commodity is flagged as a kit, used for bill of materials tracking and inventory management.']
  last_modified_by varchar [note: 'Username or identifier of the user who most recently modified the commodity record, supporting audit trail and data governance requirements.']
  last_modified_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Date and time when the commodity record was most recently updated or modified, following organizational timestamp format.']
  manufacturer_name varchar [note: 'Name of the manufacturer or producer of the commodity, important for quality assurance, traceability, and compliance with donor or regulatory requirements.']
  manufacturer_part_number varchar [note: 'Manufacturer-assigned part number or model identifier for the commodity, used for precise specification matching and procurement accuracy.']
  minimum_order_quantity decimal(15,2) [note: 'Minimum quantity that must be ordered from suppliers per procurement transaction, driven by vendor requirements or economic order quantity considerations.']
  name varchar [note: 'Full descriptive name of the humanitarian commodity or relief item as it appears in procurement documents and distribution manifests.']
  procurement_lead_time_days varchar [note: 'Average number of days required from purchase order issuance to commodity receipt at warehouse, used for supply chain planning and emergency response preparedness.']
  quality_certification varchar [note: 'Quality certifications or standards compliance held by the commodity (e.g., ISO, WHO prequalification, FDA approval, CE marking), ensuring safety and efficacy for humanitarian use.']
  reorder_point_quantity decimal(15,2) [note: 'Inventory level threshold that triggers automatic reorder or procurement action to maintain adequate stock for field operations and emergency response.']
  shelf_life_days varchar [note: 'Number of days the commodity remains usable and safe from the date of manufacture or receipt, critical for inventory rotation and distribution planning.']
  special_handling_instructions varchar [note: 'Free-text instructions for special handling, storage, or transport requirements not captured in structured fields, including fragility, orientation, stacking limits, or security considerations.']
  sphere_compliant_flag boolean [note: 'Indicates whether the commodity meets Sphere Humanitarian Charter and Minimum Standards for quality, safety, and appropriateness in humanitarian response.']
  standard_unit_cost decimal(15,2) [note: 'Standard or average cost per unit of measure for the commodity, used for budgeting, procurement planning, and financial reporting. Currency is organizational default unless otherwise specified.']
  storage_humidity_max_percent decimal(5,2) [note: 'Maximum relative humidity percentage allowed in storage environment to prevent commodity degradation, mold, or spoilage.']
  storage_temperature_max_celsius decimal(5,2) [note: 'Maximum storage temperature in degrees Celsius required to maintain commodity integrity and safety, particularly critical for medical supplies and cold-chain items.']
  storage_temperature_min_celsius decimal(5,2) [note: 'Minimum storage temperature in degrees Celsius required to maintain commodity integrity and safety, particularly critical for medical supplies and cold-chain items.']
  subcategory varchar [note: 'Secondary classification providing finer granularity within the primary commodity category (e.g., within WASH: hygiene kits, water purification tablets, sanitation supplies).']
  ultra_cold_chain_required_flag boolean [note: 'Whether ultra-cold chain storage (-60 to -80C) is required.']
  unit_of_measure varchar [note: 'Standard unit of measure used for inventory tracking, procurement, and distribution of the commodity (e.g., each, box, carton, pallet, kg, liter, meter, set, kit, dose). [ENUM-REF-CANDIDATE: each|box|carton|pallet|kg|liter|meter|set|kit|dose|vial|bottle|bag|roll — 14 candidates stripped; promote to reference product]']
  vaccine_flag boolean [note: 'Boolean flag indicating whether the vaccine condition applies.']
  vaccine_type varchar [note: 'Type of vaccine (e.g., Pentavalent, PCV13, Rotavirus, MR, BCG, OPV, IPV, HPV, Yellow Fever).']
  volume_per_unit_cubic_meters decimal(12,3) [note: 'Volume in cubic meters occupied by a single unit of the commodity, used for warehouse space planning, transport capacity optimization, and last-mile logistics.']
  vvm_type varchar [note: 'Classification type categorizing the vvm for this record.']
  weight_per_unit_kg decimal(12,3) [note: 'Weight in kilograms of a single unit of the commodity, used for logistics planning, freight cost calculation, and warehouse capacity management.']
  who_pq_reference varchar [note: 'WHO Prequalification reference number for this commodity']
  who_pq_reference_number varchar [note: 'WHO Prequalification reference number.']
  who_pq_status varchar [note: 'Current status indicator for the who pq workflow state.']

  Note: 'Supply item or commodity managed in humanitarian logistics. Source systems: ICON procurement (UNICEF Supply Division), SAP MM, WFP LESS. Systems-of-record: SAP MM, ICON, Supply Division catalogue. Framework: IPSAS 12 / WHO Essential Medicines List / UNICEF Supply Catalogue.'
}

Table supply.warehouse {
  warehouse_id varchar [pk, note: 'Unique identifier for the warehouse facility in the humanitarian supply network.']

Business justification: Warehouses in foreign jurisdictions require local operating permits, customs bonds, and facility registrations. Real business process: country office compliance tracking for operational authority.']

  cold_chain_capacity_liters decimal(12,2) [note: 'Total cold chain storage capacity in liters at +2 to +8 C']

  customs_bonded boolean [note: 'Indicates whether the warehouse operates as a customs bonded facility allowing duty-free storage of international goods.']
  decommissioning_date date [note: 'Date when the warehouse facility was decommissioned and ceased operations, if applicable.']
  emergency_access_24_7 boolean [note: 'Indicates whether the warehouse can be accessed 24 hours a day, 7 days a week for emergency response operations.']
  evm_assessment_date date [note: 'Date of last Effective Vaccine Management assessment.']
  evm_score decimal(5,2) [note: 'Effective Vaccine Management assessment score.']
  facility_type varchar [note: 'Classification of the warehouse based on its role in the humanitarian supply network.']
  forklift_capacity_kg varchar [note: 'Maximum lifting capacity of forklifts available at the warehouse, measured in kilograms.']
  freezer_capacity_liters decimal(12,2) [note: 'Total freezer storage capacity in liters (-20C).']
  gis_accuracy_meters decimal(8,2) [note: 'Accuracy of the GIS coordinates measured in meters, indicating the precision of the location data.']
  hazmat_certified boolean [note: 'Indicates whether the warehouse is certified to store hazardous materials according to international standards.']
  last_inspection_date date [note: 'Date of the most recent safety, security, or compliance inspection of the warehouse facility.']
  latitude decimal(10,7) [note: 'Geographic latitude coordinate of the warehouse facility in decimal degrees for GIS mapping and logistics planning.']
  loading_docks_count varchar [note: 'Number of loading docks available at the warehouse for receiving and dispatching cargo.']
  longitude decimal(10,7) [note: 'Geographic longitude coordinate of the warehouse facility in decimal degrees for GIS mapping and logistics planning.']
  managing_entity varchar [note: 'Type of entity responsible for the day-to-day management and operation of the warehouse facility.']
  modified_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Timestamp when this warehouse record was last modified in the system.']
  name varchar [note: 'Official name of the warehouse facility as recognized by the organization and partners.']
  notes varchar [note: 'Additional notes, comments, or special considerations regarding the warehouse facility and its operations.']
  operational_hours varchar [note: 'Standard operating hours of the warehouse facility including days and times of operation.']
  operational_status varchar [note: 'Current operational state of the warehouse facility in its lifecycle.']
  ownership_type varchar [note: 'Legal ownership or usage arrangement for the warehouse facility.']
  postal_code varchar [note: 'Postal or ZIP code for the warehouse location.']
  remote_temperature_monitoring_flag boolean [note: 'Whether the warehouse has remote/IoT temperature monitoring.']
  security_level varchar [note: 'Security classification level of the warehouse based on threat assessment and protection measures in place.']
  storage_capacity_m3 decimal(12,2) [note: 'Total storage capacity of the warehouse measured in cubic meters (m³).']
  storage_capacity_pallets varchar [note: 'Total storage capacity of the warehouse measured in standard pallet positions.']
  temperature_controlled boolean [note: 'Indicates whether the warehouse has temperature control capability for cold-chain commodities.']
  temperature_range_max_c decimal(5,2) [note: 'Maximum temperature that can be maintained in the warehouse for cold-chain storage, measured in degrees Celsius.']
  temperature_range_min_c decimal(5,2) [note: 'Minimum temperature that can be maintained in the warehouse for cold-chain storage, measured in degrees Celsius.']
  ultra_cold_capacity_liters decimal(12,2) [note: 'Ultra-cold storage capacity in liters (-60 to -80C).']
  vaccine_storage_certified_flag boolean [note: 'Whether warehouse is certified for vaccine storage']
  vaccine_storage_tier varchar [note: 'Tier in vaccine supply chain (National, Regional, District, Service Delivery Point).']
  wms_system_name varchar [note: 'Name of the warehouse management system software used to track inventory and operations at this facility.']

  Note: 'Physical warehouse or storage facility for humanitarian supplies. Source systems: SAP WM, WFP LESS, UNICEF Supply Division systems.'
}

  psea_policy_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to safeguarding.psea_policy.   address_line_2 varchar [note: 'Second line of the vendor\'s primary business address, typically containing building, suite, or unit information.']
  bank_account_number varchar [note: 'The vendor\'s primary bank account number for payment processing and fund transfers.']
  bank_name varchar [note: 'Name of the financial institution where the vendor\'s primary bank account is held.']
  bank_swift_code varchar [note: 'SWIFT/BIC code of the vendor\'s bank, used for international wire transfers and cross-border payments.']

  blacklist_expiry_date date [note: 'The date when the vendor blacklist or debarment status expires, after which the vendor may be eligible for reinstatement (nullable for permanent debarments).']

  country_of_operation varchar [note: 'Primary country where the vendor operates or is registered, using ISO 3166-1 alpha-3 country code (e.g., USA, GBR, KEN).']
  created_timestamp timestamp [note: 'The timestamp when this vendor record was first created in the system, in ISO 8601 format (yyyy-MM-ddTHH:mm:ss.SSSXXX).']
  currency_code varchar [note: 'Preferred currency for transactions with the vendor, using ISO 4217 three-letter currency code (e.g., USD, EUR, GBP).']
  fleet_size varchar [note: 'Number of vehicles or transport units in the vendor\'s fleet (applicable for transport and logistics providers). Indicates capacity for humanitarian logistics operations.']
  gmp_certification_flag boolean [note: 'Boolean indicator (True/False) showing whether the vendor holds Good Manufacturing Practice (GMP) certification, required for pharmaceutical and medical supply vendors.']
  humanitarian_network_membership varchar [note: 'Comma-separated list of humanitarian logistics networks or consortia the vendor is a member of, such as WFP Logistics Hub Framework (LHF), UNHRD (UN Humanitarian Response Depot), or Logistics Cluster.']
  iso_certification varchar [note: 'Comma-separated list of ISO certifications held by the vendor, such as ISO 9001 (Quality Management), ISO 14001 (Environmental Management), or ISO 45001 (Occupational Health and Safety).']
  last_performance_review_date date [note: 'The date when the vendor\'s performance was last formally reviewed and assessed by the procurement or supply chain team.']
  last_performance_score decimal(5,2) [note: 'The most recent performance score assigned to the vendor during the last performance review, typically on a scale of 0-100 or 0-5, measuring quality, delivery, compliance, and responsiveness.']
  modified_timestamp timestamp [note: 'The timestamp when this vendor record was last modified or updated in the system, in ISO 8601 format (yyyy-MM-ddTHH:mm:ss.SSSXXX).']
  name varchar [note: 'The full legal name of the vendor, supplier, or service provider as registered with governing authorities.']
  payment_terms_days decimal(18,2) [note: 'Standard payment terms offered by the vendor, expressed as the number of days from invoice date to payment due date (e.g., 30, 60, 90 days).']
  performance_tier varchar [note: 'Classification tier based on the vendor\'s historical performance, quality, delivery reliability, and compliance with humanitarian standards. Tier 1 represents preferred vendors with excellent track records.']
  postal_code varchar [note: 'Postal or ZIP code for the vendor\'s primary business address.']
  prequalification_date date [note: 'The date when the vendor successfully completed the prequalification process and was approved for humanitarian procurement.']
  prequalification_expiry_date date [note: 'The date when the vendor\'s prequalification status expires and requires renewal or reassessment.']
  prequalification_status varchar [note: 'Indicates whether the vendor has completed and passed the organization\'s prequalification process for humanitarian procurement, ensuring compliance with quality, ethical, and operational standards.']
  primary_contact_email varchar [note: 'Primary email address for the vendor contact person used for procurement communications, purchase orders, and supply chain coordination.']
  primary_contact_name varchar [note: 'Full name of the primary contact person at the vendor organization for procurement and supply chain coordination.']
  primary_contact_phone varchar [note: 'Primary telephone number for the vendor contact person, including country code, used for urgent procurement and logistics coordination.']
  registration_date date [note: 'The date when the vendor was first registered in the organization\'s vendor management system.']
  state_province varchar [note: 'State, province, or administrative region where the vendor\'s primary business address is located.']
  tax_identification_number varchar [note: 'The vendor\'s tax identification number (TIN), VAT number, or equivalent tax registration identifier issued by the country of operation.']
  transport_modes_offered varchar [note: 'Comma-separated list of transportation modes offered by the vendor (applicable for logistics and transport providers), such as air freight, sea freight, road transport, rail transport, or last-mile delivery.']
  un_vendor_number varchar [note: 'The unique vendor registration number assigned by the United Nations Global Marketplace (UNGM) for vendors participating in UN procurement.']
  vendor_status varchar [note: 'Current lifecycle status of the vendor in the procurement system, indicating whether the vendor is eligible for new contracts and purchase orders.']
  vendor_type varchar [note: 'Classification of the vendor based on the primary service or product category they provide in the humanitarian supply chain. [ENUM-REF-CANDIDATE: commodity_supplier|manufacturer|freight_forwarder|clearing_agent|trucking_company|air_cargo_operator|last_mile_delivery_partner|warehouse_operator|service_provider|customs_broker|cold_chain_provider — promote to reference product]']
  warehouse_capacity_sqm decimal(12,2) [note: 'Total warehouse storage capacity in square meters available from the vendor (applicable for warehouse operators and logistics providers).']
  who_pq_manufacturer_flag boolean [note: 'Whether this vendor is a WHO-prequalified vaccine manufacturer.']

  Note: 'Master record for all suppliers, vendors, service providers, and logistics operators engaged in humanitarian procurement and supply chain operations. Covers commodity suppliers, international manufacturers, freight forwarders, clearing agents, trucking companies, air cargo operators, and last-mile delivery partners. Captures vendor registration details, country of operation, commodity categories supplied, transport modes offered (where applicable), pre-qualification status, performance tier, fleet size (for transport providers), blacklist/debarment flags, UN vendor registration number, compliance certifications (e.g., ISO, GMP), and humanitarian logistics network membership (e.g., WFP LHF, UNHRD). SSOT for all supplier and service provider identity within the supply domain.'
}

Table supply.goods_receipt {

  fund_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to donor.donor_fund. Business justification: Goods receipts must be attributed to the financing donor fund for fund utilization reporting and donor visibility compliance. The existing award_id covers grant attribution but not donor fund restrict']
  intervention_id varchar [note: 'Reference to the humanitarian program or project for which these goods are intended. Links supply chain to program delivery.']
  needs_assessment_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to beneficiary.needs_assessment. Business justification: Goods receipts fulfill commodities identified in needs assessments. Closes the loop from assessment to procurement to delivery. Essential for needs-based accountability, procurement justification audi']
  partner_org_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to partnership.partner_org. Business justification: Goods often received by partner organizations at field locations. Establishes custody accountability, validates against partnership agreement authorized locations, supports audit trails for partner-ma']
  project_site_id varchar [note: 'Reference to the field project site if goods were received directly at a program location rather than a central warehouse.']
  purchase_order_id varchar [note: 'Reference to the purchase order against which goods are being received. Links this receipt to the originating procurement document.']
  vendor_id varchar [note: 'Identifier of the supplier or vendor who delivered the goods. Used for three-way matching and vendor performance tracking.']
  warehouse_id varchar [note: 'Identifier of the warehouse or field storage location where goods were physically received.']
  waybill_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to supply.waybill. Business justification: Goods receipts are recorded when commodities arrive at a warehouse. The waybill is the shipping document that accompanies the delivery. The goods_receipt table has delivery_note_number (STRING) which ']
  batch_number varchar [note: 'Manufacturer batch or lot number for the received commodity, critical for traceability and recall management, especially for medical supplies and food items.']
  cold_chain_intact_flag boolean [note: 'Whether the cold chain was maintained during transport.']
  condition_on_arrival varchar [note: 'Assessment of the physical condition of the goods upon receipt. Good indicates acceptable condition, damaged indicates physical damage, expired indicates past expiry date, partial_damage indicates some units damaged, quality_issue indicates substandard quality.']
  created_timestamp timestamp [note: 'System timestamp when this goods receipt record was first created in the database. Used for audit trail and data lineage.']
  currency_code varchar [note: 'Three-letter ISO 4217 currency code for the cost amounts (e.g., USD, EUR, GBP).']
  customs_clearance_date date [note: 'Date on which the goods cleared customs and were released for delivery to the warehouse or field location.']
  customs_cleared boolean [note: 'Indicates whether the goods have cleared customs and import formalities. True if cleared, False if pending customs clearance.']
  discrepancy_flag boolean [note: 'Indicates whether there was a discrepancy between ordered and received quantities or condition. True if discrepancy exists, False if receipt matches order exactly.']
  discrepancy_notes varchar [note: 'Detailed notes explaining any discrepancies in quantity, quality, or condition between what was ordered and what was received. Includes reasons for rejection or partial acceptance.']
  donor_visibility_flag boolean [note: 'Indicates whether this goods receipt should be included in donor reporting and visibility dashboards. True for donor-funded procurements.']
  expiry_date date [note: 'Manufacturer expiry or best-before date for perishable commodities, medical supplies, and food items. Critical for FEFO (First Expired First Out) inventory management.']
  freight_charges decimal(15,2) [note: 'Transportation and freight costs associated with this goods receipt, if separately tracked.']
  inspection_required boolean [note: 'Indicates whether the received goods require formal quality inspection before being released to inventory. True for medical supplies, food items, and high-value commodities.']
  inspection_status varchar [note: 'Status of the quality inspection process. Pending indicates inspection not yet completed, passed indicates goods approved for use, failed indicates goods rejected, waived indicates inspection was not required.']
  lot_number varchar [note: 'Supplier or donor lot number for the received commodity, used for tracking and quality control purposes.']
  manufacturing_date date [note: 'Date on which the commodity was manufactured, used to calculate shelf life and ensure quality standards.']
  modified_timestamp timestamp [note: 'System timestamp when this goods receipt record was last modified. Used for audit trail and change tracking.']
  notes varchar [note: 'General notes and comments about the goods receipt, including special handling instructions, observations, or contextual information.']
  purchase_order_line_code varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to supply.purchase_order_line. Business justification: goods_receipt already has purchase_order_id (linking to the PO header) but lacks purchase_order_line_id (linking to the specific line item). In procurement, goods are received against specific PO line']
  quality_check_status varchar [note: 'Current status indicator for the quality check workflow state.']
  quantity_ordered decimal(15,3) [note: 'The quantity of the commodity that was originally ordered on the purchase order or expected from the donation.']
  quantity_received decimal(15,3) [note: 'The actual quantity of the commodity physically received and accepted at the warehouse or field location.']
  quantity_rejected decimal(15,3) [note: 'The quantity of the commodity that was rejected upon receipt due to damage, expiry, or quality issues.']
  receipt_date date [note: 'The date on which the goods were physically received at the warehouse or field location. This is the principal business event date for this transaction.']
  receipt_document_number varchar [note: 'The externally-known unique document number for this goods receipt, typically generated by the ERP system (e.g., SAP goods receipt document number).']
  receipt_number varchar [note: 'Count or number of receipt items associated with this record.']
  receipt_status varchar [note: 'Current lifecycle status of the goods receipt transaction. Draft indicates pending finalization, posted indicates completed and inventory updated, reversed indicates a correction was applied, cancelled indicates the receipt was voided.']
  receipt_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Precise date and time when the goods receipt was recorded in the system, including time zone information.']
  serial_number varchar [note: 'Unique serial number for individually tracked items such as medical equipment, vehicles, or high-value assets.']
  storage_location_code varchar [note: 'Specific storage location or bin within the warehouse where the received goods were placed. Used for precise inventory tracking.']
  total_cost decimal(15,2) [note: 'Total cost of the goods received, calculated as quantity received multiplied by unit cost. Used for accounts payable and budget tracking.']
  unit_cost decimal(15,2) [note: 'Cost per unit of the received commodity as stated on the purchase order or donation valuation. Used for inventory valuation and financial accounting.']
  unit_of_measure varchar [note: 'The unit in which the commodity quantity is measured. EA=Each, KG=Kilogram, LT=Liter, MT=Metric Ton, BX=Box, CS=Case, PK=Pack. [ENUM-REF-CANDIDATE: EA|KG|LT|MT|BX|CS|PK — 7 candidates stripped; promote to reference product]']
  vvm_status_on_arrival varchar [note: 'Vaccine Vial Monitor stage observed on arrival (VVM stage 1-4).']

  Note: 'Transactional record capturing the physical receipt of commodities at a warehouse or field location against a purchase order or in-kind donation. Records receipt date, receiving warehouse, commodity, quantity received, condition on arrival (good/damaged/expired), batch/lot number, expiry date, receiving officer, discrepancy notes, and SAP goods receipt document number. Triggers inventory update and initiates three-way matching for AP payment processing.'
}

Table supply.inventory_balance {
  inventory_balance_id varchar [pk, note: 'Unique identifier for the inventory balance record. Primary key for this entity.']
  award_id varchar [note: 'Reference to the donor grant funding this inventory. Used for fund accounting and donor reporting. Links to grant master data.']

  fund_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to donor.donor_fund. Business justification: Inventory balances for donor-restricted stock must be attributed to the donor fund to enforce restrictions, prevent co-mingling, and report fund utilization. The donor_restriction_flag plain attribute']
  intervention_id varchar [note: 'Reference to the humanitarian program or project for which this inventory is allocated or reserved. Links to program master data.']
  warehouse_id varchar [note: 'Reference to the warehouse or storage location where the commodity is held. Links to warehouse master data.']
  batch_number varchar [note: 'Manufacturer batch or lot number for the commodity. Used for quality control, recall management, and traceability.']
  country_code varchar [note: 'Three-letter ISO 3166-1 alpha-3 country code for the warehouse location. Used for geographic reporting and OCHA SitRep aggregation.']
  created_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Timestamp when this inventory balance record was first created in the system. Used for audit trail and data lineage.']
  currency_code varchar [note: 'Three-letter ISO 4217 currency code for the inventory valuation. Used for multi-currency financial reporting.']
  donor_restriction_flag boolean [note: 'Indicates whether this inventory is subject to donor-imposed restrictions on use, distribution, or geographic allocation. True if restricted, False if unrestricted.']
  expiration_date date [note: 'Expiration or best-before date for perishable commodities such as medical supplies, food, or nutrition items. Used for FEFO (First Expired First Out) distribution planning.']
  in_kind_donation_flag boolean [note: 'Indicates whether this inventory was received as an in-kind donation rather than purchased. True if in-kind donation, False if purchased. Used for donor reporting and GAAP compliance.']
  last_movement_date date [note: 'Date of the most recent inventory transaction (receipt, issue, transfer, or adjustment) for this balance record. Used for aging analysis and pipeline monitoring.']
  last_physical_count_date date [note: 'Date of the most recent physical inventory count or cycle count for this commodity at this warehouse. Used for inventory accuracy tracking and audit compliance.']
  last_updated_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Timestamp when this inventory balance record was last modified. Used for audit trail and change tracking.']
  maximum_stock_level decimal(18,3) [note: 'Maximum stock capacity or target level for this commodity at this warehouse. Used for inventory optimization and storage planning.']
  notes varchar [note: 'Free-text notes or comments about this inventory balance record. Used for documenting special conditions, quality issues, or operational context.']
  pipeline_status varchar [note: 'Current status of the inventory balance in the commodity pipeline. Used for OCHA cluster reporting and pipeline gap analysis.']
  quantity_available decimal(18,3) [note: 'Net quantity available for new distribution allocations. Calculated as quantity_on_hand minus quantity_reserved minus quantity_quarantined.']
  quantity_in_transit decimal(18,3) [note: 'Quantity currently in transit to this warehouse from suppliers or other warehouses. Not yet available for distribution.']
  quantity_on_hand decimal(18,3) [note: 'Current physical stock quantity available in the warehouse. Represents the total inventory balance at the snapshot date.']
  quantity_quarantined decimal(18,3) [note: 'Quantity held in quarantine due to quality issues, expiration concerns, or pending inspection. Not available for distribution.']
  quantity_reserved decimal(18,3) [note: 'Quantity allocated or reserved for planned distribution events but not yet dispatched. Reduces available stock for new allocations.']
  reorder_level decimal(18,3) [note: 'Minimum stock level threshold that triggers a replenishment order. Used for pipeline management and pre-positioning decisions.']
  snapshot_date date [note: 'Date for which this inventory balance snapshot is recorded. Supports historical trend analysis and time-series reporting.']
  storage_condition varchar [note: 'Required storage condition for the commodity. Used for warehouse capacity planning and quality assurance.']
  total_valuation decimal(18,2) [note: 'Total monetary value of the inventory balance. Calculated as quantity_on_hand multiplied by unit_cost. Used for financial statements and donor reporting.']
  unit_cost decimal(18,4) [note: 'Standard or average cost per unit of the commodity. Used for inventory valuation and financial reporting.']
  unit_of_measure varchar [note: 'Standard unit of measure for the commodity quantity. Used for consistent reporting and distribution planning. [ENUM-REF-CANDIDATE: EA|KG|LTR|MTR|BOX|CARTON|PALLET|DOSE|VIAL|TABLET|KIT — 11 candidates stripped; promote to reference product]']
  vvm_current_stage varchar [note: 'Current VVM stage for vaccine inventory (VVM stage 1-4).']
  vvm_stage varchar [note: 'Current VVM stage of inventory (1-4) for vaccine commodities']
  warehouse_location varchar [note: 'Geographic location of the warehouse including city, region, or country. Used for logistics planning and SitRep reporting.']

  Note: 'Current and historical stock balance records for each commodity at each warehouse location. Captures commodity, warehouse, stock quantity on hand, quantity reserved for distribution, quantity in transit, quantity quarantined, reorder level, maximum stock level, last physical count date, and stock valuation. Supports commodity pipeline management, pre-positioning decisions, and OCHA cluster reporting on pipeline gaps.'
}

  source_warehouse_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to supply.warehouse. Business justification: Stock movements track inventory transfers between warehouses or within a warehouse. The source_location_id (BIGINT) currently holds the origin warehouse reference. Renaming to source_warehouse_id and ']
  team_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to field.field_team. Business justification: Stock movements (issues, transfers, adjustments) at field level are authorized and executed by field teams. Linking stock_movement to field_team enables field-level inventory accountability and audit ']
  vendor_id varchar [note: 'Identifier of the external supplier or vendor from whom goods were received (applicable for movement_type = receipt). Null for internal movements.']
  authorizing_officer_name varchar [note: 'Full name of the staff member who authorized this stock movement. Denormalized for reporting and audit trail readability.']
  batch_number varchar [note: 'Manufacturer or supplier batch/lot number for the commodity. Critical for traceability, recall management, and quality control, especially for medical supplies and food items.']
  carrier_name varchar [note: 'Name of the transport carrier or logistics service provider responsible for moving the commodity (applicable for transfers and receipts). Null for internal hand-carry movements.']
  count_team_reference varchar [note: 'Reference identifier for the physical inventory count team or cycle that generated this movement record (applicable only for movement_type = physical_count). Links to physical inventory count documentation.']
  created_timestamp timestamp [note: 'System timestamp when this stock movement record was first created in the system. Part of audit trail for data lineage and compliance.']
  currency_code varchar [note: 'Three-letter ISO 4217 currency code for the cost fields. Typically the organization\'s functional currency (e.g., USD, EUR, GBP).']
  expiry_date date [note: 'Expiration or best-before date of the commodity batch being moved. Critical for FEFO (First Expired First Out) inventory management, especially for medical supplies, food items, and time-sensitive NFIs.']
  in_kind_donation_flag boolean [note: 'Indicates whether this commodity was received as an in-kind donation (True) or procured through cash purchase (False). Critical for donor reporting and IATI transparency.']
  inspection_date date [note: 'Date on which quality inspection was performed on the commodity. Null if inspection was not required or waived.']
  inspector_name varchar [note: 'Name of the staff member or third-party inspector who performed the quality inspection. Null if inspection was not required or waived.']
  last_modified_timestamp timestamp [note: 'System timestamp when this stock movement record was last updated. Part of audit trail for data lineage and compliance.']
  movement_date date [note: 'Business date on which the stock movement occurred or is scheduled to occur. This is the operational event date, distinct from system audit timestamps.']
  movement_number varchar [note: 'Externally-known unique reference number for this stock movement transaction, used for audit trail and cross-system reconciliation. Format typically includes movement type prefix and sequential number.']
  movement_status varchar [note: 'Current lifecycle status of the stock movement transaction. Tracks workflow state from initiation through completion or cancellation. [ENUM-REF-CANDIDATE: draft|pending_approval|approved|in_transit|completed|cancelled|rejected — 7 candidates stripped; promote to reference product]']
  movement_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Precise date and time when the physical stock movement was executed or recorded. Used for detailed audit trail and sequence reconstruction.']
  movement_type varchar [note: 'Classification of the stock movement transaction. Receipt = goods received into warehouse; Issue = goods distributed/issued out; Transfer = movement between warehouses; Adjustment = correction entry; Physical_count = reconciliation from physical inventory count; Loss = documented loss/damage; Write_off = removal from inventory due to expiry/obsolescence. [ENUM-REF-CANDIDATE: receipt|issue|transfer|adjustment|physical_count|loss|write_off — 7 candidates stripped; promote to reference product]']
  notes varchar [note: 'Free-text notes or comments about this stock movement transaction. Used to capture additional context, special handling instructions, or operational observations.']
  quality_inspection_status varchar [note: 'Result of quality inspection performed on the commodity at the time of receipt or movement. Pending = inspection not yet completed; Passed = meets quality standards; Failed = rejected; Waived = inspection requirement waived for emergency; Not_required = commodity type does not require inspection.']
  quantity decimal(18,4) [note: 'Quantity of the commodity being moved in the transaction. Always positive; movement direction is determined by movement_type. Precision supports fractional units for medical supplies and bulk commodities.']
  reason_code varchar [note: 'Standardized code explaining the business reason for the stock movement. Critical for loss analysis, donor reporting, and pipeline management. Normal_receipt = routine procurement; Emergency_receipt = rapid response; Beneficiary_distribution = direct aid delivery; Program_issue = program consumption; Inter_warehouse_transfer = logistics optimization; Count_adjustment = inventory reconciliation; Expiry/Damage/Theft/Loss_in_transit/Obsolescence = loss categories. [ENUM-REF-CANDIDATE: normal_receipt|emergency_receipt|beneficiary_distribution|program_issue|inter_warehouse_transfer|count_adjustment|expiry|damage|theft|loss_in_transit|obsolescence — 11 candidates stripped; promote to reference product]']
  reason_description varchar [note: 'Free-text detailed explanation of the reason for the stock movement, especially for adjustments, losses, and write-offs. Provides context beyond the standardized reason code.']
  reference_document_number varchar [note: 'Unique number of the source document (GRN, waybill, distribution order, count sheet, etc.) that authorizes or records this movement. Provides full audit trail linkage.']
  reference_document_type varchar [note: 'Type of source document that authorizes or records this stock movement. GRN = Goods Received Note; Waybill = transport document; Distribution_order = beneficiary distribution plan; Count_sheet = physical inventory count; Adjustment_memo = correction authorization; Loss_report = documented loss/damage; Write_off_authorization = disposal approval. [ENUM-REF-CANDIDATE: GRN|waybill|distribution_order|count_sheet|adjustment_memo|loss_report|write_off_authorization — 7 candidates stripped; promote to reference product]']
  serial_number varchar [note: 'Unique serial number for individually tracked items such as medical equipment, vehicles, or high-value assets. Null for bulk commodities.']
  total_cost decimal(18,2) [note: 'Total cost of this stock movement transaction (quantity × unit_cost), in the organization\'s functional currency. Used for inventory valuation, grant expenditure tracking, and donor reporting. Confidential business data.']
  tracking_number varchar [note: 'Carrier-provided tracking number for the shipment associated with this stock movement. Enables real-time visibility of in-transit commodities.']
  transport_mode varchar [note: 'Mode of transportation used for this stock movement (applicable for transfers and receipts). Road = truck/vehicle; Air = aircraft; Sea = ship/barge; Rail = train; Pipeline = bulk liquid/gas; Hand_carry = manual transport for small quantities.']
  unit_cost decimal(18,4) [note: 'Cost per unit of the commodity at the time of this movement, in the organization\'s functional currency. Used for inventory valuation and donor financial reporting. Confidential business data.']
  unit_of_measure varchar [note: 'Unit of measure for the quantity field. Standardized to organizational commodity catalog units. [ENUM-REF-CANDIDATE: piece|box|carton|pallet|kg|liter|meter|dose|kit|set — 10 candidates stripped; promote to reference product]']
  vvm_status_at_movement timestamp [note: 'VVM stage at time of stock movement.']

  Note: 'Transactional log of all inventory movements including receipts, issues, transfers between warehouses, adjustments, physical count reconciliations, losses, and write-offs. Each record captures movement type (receipt/issue/transfer/adjustment/physical_count/loss/write-off), commodity, source location, destination location, quantity, movement date, reference document (GRN/waybill/distribution order/count sheet), reason code, authorizing officer, and count team reference (for physical count adjustments). Provides full commodity pipeline audit trail for donor reporting, IATI transparency, inventory accuracy management, and loss detection.'
}

  geographic_coverage_country varchar [note: 'Three-letter ISO country code where the distribution will take place.']

  planned_end_date date [note: 'Scheduled date when distribution activities are planned to be completed.']
  planned_quantity decimal [note: 'The total quantity of this commodity planned for distribution under this plan. Belongs to the allocation, not the plan (which covers many commodities) nor the commodity master (which is quantity-agnostic).']

  risk_level varchar [note: 'Overall risk assessment level for this distribution plan considering security, logistics, and operational challenges.']
  sdg_alignment varchar [note: 'Sustainable Development Goals that this distribution plan supports (e.g., SDG 1: No Poverty, SDG 2: Zero Hunger).']
  security_clearance_required boolean [note: 'Indicates whether security clearance or authorization is required before distribution can proceed in the target area.']
  target_beneficiary_count varchar [note: 'Planned number of individual beneficiaries or households to be reached by this distribution plan.']
  target_beneficiary_count_for_commodity varchar [note: 'Number of beneficiaries targeted to receive this specific commodity under this plan. May differ from the plan-level beneficiary count (e.g., only children under 5 receive a specific supplement). Belongs to the allocation, not the plan total nor the commodity master.']
  target_household_count varchar [note: 'Planned number of households to receive assistance under this distribution plan.']
  unit_of_measure varchar [note: 'The unit in which planned_quantity is expressed for this commodity in this plan (e.g., MT for food, DOSE for vaccines, CARTON for NFIs). Varies by commodity-plan combination and cannot reside on the commodity master alone.']
  vaccine_campaign_flag boolean [note: 'Whether this distribution plan is for a vaccination campaign.']

  Note: 'Master plan for the distribution of humanitarian commodities to beneficiaries at field locations. Captures distribution plan name, target program, geographic coverage (country/admin levels), planned distribution date range, target beneficiary count, commodity list with planned quantities per beneficiary, distribution modality (direct/voucher/cash-in-kind), responsible field office, and approval status. Links supply chain planning to field operations and MEL targets.'
}
