Table safeguarding.incident {
  incident_id varchar [pk, note: 'Primary key.']
  award_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to grant.award. Business justification: Donor agreements (USAID, DFID, UN) mandate incident notification within 24-72 hours tied to the specific award. Compliance officers must identify which donor to notify and which award\'s reporting obli']
  country_id varchar [note: 'FK to the country where incident occurred.']
  country_office_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to field.country_office. Business justification: Country offices are the accountable entity for safeguarding incident management, donor notifications, and regulatory reporting. NGO accountability frameworks require country office directors to own in']
  emergency_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to field.emergency. Business justification: PSEA/safeguarding incident reporting requires linking incidents to the emergency context in which they occurred. Donor notifications, IASC PSEA compliance reports, and cluster coordination updates all']
  focal_point_id varchar [note: 'FK to the assigned focal point.']
  fund_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to donor.donor_fund. Business justification: NGO donor notification process: when an incident triggers donor_notification_required_flag, staff must identify which donor fund is implicated to fulfill contractual notification obligations. This FK ']
  intervention_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to program.intervention. Business justification: Safeguarding incidents must be attributed to the intervention in which they occurred for CHS compliance, donor incident reporting, and intervention-level safeguarding performance monitoring. This is a']
  partner_org_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to partnership.partner_org. Business justification: Safeguarding incidents frequently involve implementing partner organizations. Donor reporting, PSEA investigations, and accountability frameworks require identifying which partner org is implicated. T']
  project_site_id varchar [note: 'FK to the project site where incident occurred.']
  psea_policy_id varchar [note: 'FK to the governing PSEA policy.']
  reporting_channel_id varchar [note: 'FK to the channel through which the incident was reported.']
  subaward_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to grant.subaward. Business justification: Safeguarding incidents frequently occur during partner-implemented subawards. The subaward governs partner flow-down obligations and reporting duties. Linking incident to subaward enables partner acco']
  team_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to field.field_team. Business justification: Safeguarding incidents are often associated with specific field teams whose members are involved as witnesses, alleged perpetrators, or first responders. Linking enables team-level incident pattern an']
  category varchar [note: 'Category classification of the incident.']
  closure_date date [note: 'Date the incident was closed.']
  closure_reason varchar [note: 'Reason for closure.']
  confidentiality_level varchar [note: 'Confidentiality classification level.']
  created_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Record creation timestamp.']
  donor_notification_date date [note: 'Date donor was notified.']
  donor_notification_required_flag boolean [note: 'Whether donor notification is required.']
  incident_date date [note: 'Date the incident occurred.']
  incident_number varchar [note: 'Unique incident reference number.']
  incident_status varchar [note: 'Current status (Reported, Under Investigation, Closed, Referred).']
  incident_type varchar [note: 'Type of incident (SEA, Sexual Harassment, Child Abuse, Other Misconduct).']
  involves_minor_flag boolean [note: 'Whether the incident involves a minor.']
  last_modified_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Record last modified timestamp.']
  lessons_learned varchar [note: 'Lessons learned from the incident.']
  location_description varchar [note: 'Description of incident location.']
  notes varchar [note: 'Additional notes. PII protected.']
  perpetrator_count varchar [note: 'Number of alleged perpetrators.']
  referred_to_authorities_flag boolean [note: 'Whether the incident was referred to local authorities.']
  reported_date date [note: 'Date the incident was reported.']
  safeguarding_incident_description varchar [note: 'Narrative description of the incident. PII protected.']
  severity_level varchar [note: 'Severity level (Critical, High, Medium, Low).']
  survivor_count varchar [note: 'Number of survivors involved.']

  Note: 'SSOT for safeguarding incidents including PSEA (Protection from Sexual Exploitation and Abuse), sexual harassment, child protection violations, and abuse of power. Distinct from compliance.compliance_incident which covers regulatory/financial compliance breaches.'
}

Table safeguarding.investigation {
  investigation_id varchar [pk, note: 'Primary key.']
  award_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to grant.award. Business justification: Donor notification of safeguarding investigations is a distinct compliance obligation from incident notification under most major donor frameworks. Linking investigation directly to award enables inve']
  case_record_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to beneficiary.case_record. Business justification: When a safeguarding investigation involves a registered beneficiary, investigators must access the full case record for context (prior incidents, service history, vulnerability profile). This link sup']
  incident_id varchar [note: 'FK to the safeguarding incident.']
  partner_org_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to partnership.partner_org. Business justification: Safeguarding investigations frequently target implementing partner organizations. Donor-mandated investigation reports must identify the partner org under investigation. This link enables partner acco']
  psea_policy_id varchar [note: 'FK to the governing PSEA policy.']
  actual_completion_date date [note: 'Actual completion date.']
  conclusion varchar [note: 'Investigation conclusion (Substantiated, Unsubstantiated, Inconclusive).']
  confidentiality_level varchar [note: 'Confidentiality classification.']
  created_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Record creation timestamp.']
  evidence_collected_flag boolean [note: 'Whether evidence was collected.']
  external_referral_flag boolean [note: 'Whether referred to external body.']
  findings_summary varchar [note: 'Summary of investigation findings. PII protected.']
  initiation_date date [note: 'Date investigation was initiated.']
  investigation_number varchar [note: 'Unique investigation reference.']
  investigation_status varchar [note: 'Current status (Initiated, In Progress, Completed, Closed).']
  investigation_type varchar [note: 'Type of investigation (Internal, External, Joint).']
  last_modified_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Record last modified timestamp.']
  notes varchar [note: 'Additional notes.']
  recommendations varchar [note: 'Recommendations from the investigation.']
  report_url varchar [note: 'URL to investigation report.']
  target_completion_date date [note: 'Target date for completion.']
  witnesses_interviewed_count varchar [note: 'Number of witnesses interviewed.']

  Note: 'Formal investigation record linked to a safeguarding incident. Tracks investigation lifecycle, findings, and outcomes.'
}

Table safeguarding.investigation_action {
  investigation_action_id varchar [pk, note: 'Primary key.']
  investigation_id varchar [note: 'FK to the investigation.']
  action_description varchar [note: 'Description of the action.']
  action_status varchar [note: 'Status (Planned, In Progress, Completed, Cancelled).']
  action_type varchar [note: 'Type of action (Interview, Evidence Collection, Site Visit, Document Review).']
  completed_date date [note: 'Actual completion date.']
  confidentiality_level varchar [note: 'Confidentiality level.']
  created_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Record creation timestamp.']
  last_modified_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Record last modified timestamp.']
  notes varchar [note: 'Additional notes.']
  outcome_summary varchar [note: 'Summary of action outcome.']
  scheduled_date date [note: 'Scheduled date for the action.']

  Note: 'Individual actions taken during an investigation (interviews, evidence collection, site visits).'
}

Table safeguarding.survivor_record {
  survivor_record_id varchar [pk, note: 'Primary key.']
  incident_id varchar [note: 'FK to the safeguarding incident.']
  project_site_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to field.project_site. Business justification: Survivor records must capture the project site where the survivor was being served or where the incident occurred. Site-level safeguarding reviews, referral pathway planning, and geographic clustering']
  registrant_id varchar [note: 'FK to the beneficiary registrant if applicable.']
  age_group varchar [note: 'Age group (Child, Adolescent, Adult).']
  consent_date date [note: 'Date consent was obtained.']
  consent_obtained_flag boolean [note: 'Whether informed consent was obtained.']
  created_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Record creation timestamp.']
  disability_flag boolean [note: 'Whether the survivor has a disability.']
  displacement_status varchar [note: 'Displacement status of the survivor.']
  is_minor_flag boolean [note: 'Whether the survivor is a minor.']
  last_modified_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Record last modified timestamp.']
  notes varchar [note: 'Case notes. Highly sensitive.']
  safety_plan_in_place_flag boolean [note: 'Whether a safety plan is in place.']
  sex varchar [note: 'Sex of the survivor.']
  support_status varchar [note: 'Current support status (Active, Closed, Referred).']
  survivor_code varchar [note: 'De-identified code for the survivor.']

  Note: 'De-identified survivor record linked to a safeguarding incident. Contains only minimal demographic data needed for case management. All fields are pii_beneficiary_protected.'
}

Table safeguarding.survivor_support_plan {
  survivor_support_plan_id varchar [pk, note: 'Primary key.']
  case_record_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to beneficiary.case_record. Business justification: A survivor support plan in NGO operations is coordinated with the broader case management system. Linking to case_record enables case workers to access the full service history alongside the safeguard']
  focal_point_id varchar [note: 'FK to the assigned focal point.']
  survivor_record_id varchar [note: 'FK to the survivor record.']
  created_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Record creation timestamp.']
  last_modified_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Record last modified timestamp.']
  last_review_date date [note: 'Date of last review.']
  next_review_date date [note: 'Date of next scheduled review.']
  notes varchar [note: 'Additional notes.']
  outcome_summary varchar [note: 'Summary of outcomes achieved.']
  plan_end_date date [note: 'Plan end date.']
  plan_start_date date [note: 'Plan start date.']
  plan_status varchar [note: 'Status (Draft, Active, Completed, Closed).']
  priority_level varchar [note: 'Priority level (Critical, High, Medium, Low).']
  review_frequency varchar [note: 'How often the plan is reviewed.']
  safety_measures varchar [note: 'Safety measures in place.']
  services_required varchar [note: 'List of services required (medical, psychosocial, legal, shelter).']

  Note: 'Support plan for a survivor including services, timeline, and responsible parties.'
}

Table safeguarding.risk_assessment {
  risk_assessment_id varchar [pk, note: 'Primary key.']
  component_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to program.component. Business justification: NGO safeguarding risk assessments are scoped to specific program components (e.g., cash transfer vs. protection components carry distinct SEA risk profiles). Component-level risk assessment is require']
  country_office_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to field.country_office. Business justification: Country offices commission, review, and approve safeguarding risk assessments as part of their operational accountability. Country directors sign off on risk mitigation plans. This link enables countr']
  focal_point_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to safeguarding.focal_point. Business justification: A safeguarding risk assessment is typically conducted or overseen by the designated PSEA/safeguarding focal point responsible for the geographic area or organizational unit being assessed. Linking ris']
  project_site_id varchar [note: 'FK to the project site.']
  psea_policy_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to safeguarding.psea_policy. Business justification: A safeguarding risk assessment is conducted under the governance of a specific PSEA policy. Linking risk_assessment to psea_policy establishes which policy framework governed the assessment, enabling ']
  team_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to field.field_team. Business justification: Safeguarding risk assessments are scoped to specific field teams to evaluate deployment-level SEA and child safeguarding risks. Team composition, operating context, and beneficiary contact patterns de']
  assessment_date date [note: 'Date of assessment.']
  assessment_status varchar [note: 'Status (Draft, Completed, Approved).']
  assessment_type varchar [note: 'Type (Initial, Periodic, Triggered).']
  child_safeguarding_risk_score double [note: 'Child safeguarding risk score.']
  created_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Record creation timestamp.']
  last_modified_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Record last modified timestamp.']
  mitigation_measures varchar [note: 'Description of mitigation measures.']
  next_review_date date [note: 'Date of next scheduled review.']
  notes varchar [note: 'Additional notes.']
  overall_risk_level varchar [note: 'Overall risk level (Critical, High, Medium, Low).']
  residual_risk_level varchar [note: 'Residual risk level after mitigation.']
  sea_risk_score double [note: 'SEA-specific risk score.']
  sh_risk_score double [note: 'Sexual harassment risk score.']

  Note: 'Safeguarding risk assessment for a program, project site, or partner. Identifies SEA/SH risks and mitigation measures.'
}

Table safeguarding.reporting_channel {
  reporting_channel_id varchar [pk, note: 'Primary key.']
  country_id varchar [note: 'FK to the country.']
  country_office_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to field.country_office. Business justification: Reporting channels are established, funded, and managed by country offices. Country offices are accountable for ensuring accessible complaint mechanisms per PSEA policy. This link enables country-offi']
  focal_point_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to safeguarding.focal_point. Business justification: Each reporting channel (hotline, email, community-based mechanism) is managed and overseen by a designated safeguarding focal point. Linking reporting_channel to focal_point establishes operational ow']
  accessibility_features varchar [note: 'Accessibility features available.']
  channel_name varchar [note: 'Name of the reporting channel.']
  channel_status varchar [note: 'Status (Active, Inactive, Under Maintenance).']
  channel_type varchar [note: 'Type (Hotline, Email, In-Person, Web Form, Community Box, SMS).']
  contact_details varchar [note: 'Contact details for the channel.']
  created_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Record creation timestamp.']
  is_anonymous_flag boolean [note: 'Whether anonymous reporting is supported.']
  is_confidential_flag boolean [note: 'Whether confidentiality is guaranteed.']
  languages_supported varchar [note: 'Languages supported by this channel.']
  last_modified_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Record last modified timestamp.']
  notes varchar [note: 'Additional notes.']
  operating_hours varchar [note: 'Operating hours of the channel.']

  Note: 'Reporting channel/mechanism for safeguarding concerns (hotline, email, in-person, community-based).'
}

Table safeguarding.focal_point {
  focal_point_id varchar [pk, note: 'Primary key.']
  country_id varchar [note: 'FK to the country.']
  country_office_id varchar [note: 'Foreign key linking to field.country_office. Business justification: Safeguarding focal points are designated by and report to a specific country office. Country office management tracks focal point coverage, training compliance, and workload. This link is required for']
  contact_email varchar [note: 'Contact email for the focal point.']
  contact_phone varchar [note: 'Contact phone for the focal point.']
  created_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Record creation timestamp.']
  designation_date date [note: 'Date of designation.']
  end_date date [note: 'End date of assignment.']
  focal_point_type varchar [note: 'Type (Primary, Alternate, Regional).']
  is_active_flag boolean [note: 'Whether currently active.']
  last_modified_timestamp timestamp [note: 'Record last modified timestamp.']
  notes varchar [note: 'Additional notes.']
  training_completed_flag boolean [note: 'Whether required training is completed.']

  Note: 'Designated safeguarding/PSEA focal point responsible for a geographic area or organizational unit.'
}
